Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe","abstract":"FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We sho","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T15:40:51Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2606_20381.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当前工业界部署在Blackwell/Rubin、MI350等新硬件上的FP4预训练方案（包括NVFP4、MXFP4）默认采用非均匀的E2M1数值格式，配套随机哈达玛变换（RHT）和随机舍入稳定训练，但始终存在收敛不稳定、相对BF16有明显loss退化的问题。现有改进多围绕缩放规则、舍入策略、离群值处理做增量调整，没有回到数值格式本身的几何特性寻找根因。","importance":"该工作不是对现有FP4训练trick的增量修补，而是定位了E2M1方案训练不稳定的核心根源：非均匀网格的几何结构自带系统性舍入偏差，这一结论统一解释了此前NVFP4类方案中观察到的收敛差、全路径RHT易引发训练崩溃等现象。提出的均匀格式UFP4方案在124B MoE长程预训练上完成验证，相比充分调优的E2M1基线有更低的BF16相对loss退化，且实现开销更低，可直接落地到工业级训练管线。结论直接影响下一代低精度硬件的数值格式设计、生产级FP4训练recipe的选型，不属于经验性调参层面的改进。","technical_approach":"首先形式化定义了收缩偏差（Shrinkage Bias）：E2M1这类非均匀浮点格式的可表示值按几何间距排列，相邻值的舍入区间天然不对称——区间中点更靠近小值一端，在业界默认的就近偶舍入（RTNE，确定性舍入）规则下，落入每个区间的数的量化期望会系统性偏小，形成稳定的负舍入误差。理论分析表明，这种偏差是跨层乘性累积的，不会像零均值加性噪声那样在训练中相互抵消。此前业界选择E2M1的直觉是其非均匀结构更适配张量的重尾分布、动态范围更优，但配套使用的RHT（原本用来打散离群值、提升码本利用率）会将张量能量均匀分散，形成近似高斯的近零分布，恰好落在E2M1区间不对称性最严重的数值范围，反而放大了收缩偏差。而E1M2/INT4这类均匀格式的可表示值等间距分布，从根本上不存在几何不对称带来的偏差，且和RHT之后的近高斯分布匹配度更高，能把RHT带来的码本利用率提升转化为更稳定的量化精度。基于这一发现设计的UFP4训练recipe做了两个关键选择：一是在前向、数据梯度、权重梯度三个线性层GEMM路径上全部应用RHT，解决了此前E2M1方案中全路径RHT易引发训练崩溃的问题；二是仅在量化上游梯度dY时使用随机舍入，权重和激活量化均使用确定性RTNE，减少随机舍入带来的更新方差。和现有E2M1方案的核心区别是：现有方案始终保留E2M1格式，通过调整缩放规则、增加离群值裁剪、扩大随机舍入范围等外围策略稳训练；UFP4直接修正了格式与RHT后张量分布的错配，无需复杂的稳定化策略即可实现稳定的端到端FP4训练。","experiments":"验证覆盖三个规模的完整长程预训练：1.5B Dense、7.9B MoE、124B MoE。124B MoE上的核心结果是：经过充分调优的E2M1强基线（NVFP4类recipe）相对BF16训练存在明显的loss退化，且训练后期易出现波动；UFP4的相对loss退化显著低于E2M1基线，训练全程稳定无发散。消融实验得到几个关键结论：1）收缩偏差的累积效应：网络层数越深，E2M1带来的信号衰减越明显，均匀格式无系统性衰减；2）RHT与格式的交互：E2M1叠加RHT会增大收缩偏差、降低量化信噪比，均匀格式叠加全路径RHT反而能提升量化精度；3）随机舍入的最优位置：仅在dY路径使用随机舍入即可保证收敛，在权重或激活路径使用随机舍入不会带来额外收益，反而会增大更新方差；4）Scaling规律验证：在1B到124B的规模区间内，UFP4相对BF16的loss差距始终维持在很小的恒定值，E2M1的相对差距会随训练步数增加、模型规模扩大而被放大；5）kernel性能实测：均匀格式的量化逻辑更简单，融合kernel的运行开销比优化后的E2M1 NVFP4实现略低，不存在硬件落地的额外成本障碍。","reading_guide":"重点读第3章关于收缩偏差几何起源、跨层乘性累积的推导，这是核心洞见，区别于常规的工程trick修补。重点看图1(b)的124B MoE长程loss曲线，百B级MoE的长程训练结果比小模型实验的工业参考价值高很多。第5章的消融实验值得仔细看，特别是RHT与格式的交互、随机舍入位置的结果，可以纠正早期FP4工作中“RHT不能全路径使用、随机舍入要覆盖所有量化张量”的经验性误区。值得后续深挖的方向：非均匀的FP8格式（如E4M3）是否也存在更小量级的收缩偏差？Block量化的块大小选择会如何影响收缩偏差的累积速度？","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Production FP4 pretraining recipes deployed on latest accelerators (NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin, AMD MI350, including NVFP4 and MXFP4) universally adopt the non-uniform E2M1 data format, paired with Random Hadamard Transform (RHT) and stochastic rounding for stability. These recipes still suffer from persistent convergence issues and non-trivial loss degradation relative to BF16, even after extensive tuning of scaling rules, rounding schemes, and outlier handling. No prior work has traced these failures to fundamental geometric properties of the E2M1 format itself.","importance":"This work identifies a root cause rather than patching incremental symptoms: the geometric asymmetry of non-uniform E2M1 grids introduces a systematic rounding bias that explains previously observed training instabilities in E2M1-based FP4 pipelines. The proposed uniform-grid UFP4 recipe is validated on 124B-parameter MoE long-run pretraining, delivering lower BF16-relative loss degradation with slightly lower implementation overhead than well-tuned E2M1 baselines. The finding directly informs next-generation low-precision hardware format design and production FP4 training recipe selection, rather than adding layers of ad-hoc stabilization tricks to existing approaches.","technical_approach":"$e","experiments":"$f","reading_guide":"Prioritize Section 3 for the derivation of shrinkage bias's geometric origin and multiplicative cross-layer accumulation, which is the core conceptual contribution rather than an engineering tweak. Focus on the 124B MoE long-run loss curve in Figure 1(b), as results at this scale carry direct industrial relevance that small-model toy experiments cannot match. Read the ablations on RHT-format interaction and stochastic rounding placement in Section 5 closely—these correct common folk wisdom from early FP4 papers that RHT cannot be applied to all training paths and that stochastic rounding is required for all quantized tensors. test-time compute 不是简单增加 token 或调用次数，而要看一次干预能否修复当前样本，以及是否会把正确答案改错。这个视角对后训练中的 verifier、process reward、self-correction 数据构造也有启发：训练信号不应只奖励“多想”，还应区分 helpful fix 和 harmful flip。","technical_approach":"SeVRA 使用冻结的 Qwen3-4B reasoning model 先生成基础答案，再由一个轻量 gate 决定是否触发 active verification。active verification 不是普通续写，而是要求同一个冻结模型为候选答案构造至少两个针对性检查，例如重建方程、检查单位和边界、代回候选答案、走独立解法；只有检查失败时才修改答案。gate 的训练标签来自离线执行后的结果：一次验证是否把错误答案修好，或者是否把正确答案改错。部署时 gate 只能看到题目、初始解答和运行时可见特征，不能访问 gold answer。作者比较了三类 gate：基于 completion status、token count、finalizer use、估计难度、约束密度等廉价特征的 logistic gate；Qwen3-0.6B 4-bit QLoRA sequence classifier；Qwen3-1.7B 4-bit QLoRA sequence classifier。方法的关键不在 verifier 模型更强，而在把验证看成带收益和风险的选择性动作。","experiments":"主要实验使用冻结 Qwen3-4B。MATH500 上，SeVRA 达到 76.3% accuracy，高于 always verifying 的 75.5%，同时 post-generation tokens 减少 26.8%，并降低 harmful flips。GSM8K 的 frozen transfer 中，gate 只验证少量样本，相比 short-base solver 有提升，并且没有观察到 harmful flips。作者也做了预算匹配比较：接受初始答案、继续推理、主动验证、总是验证、增加初始 reasoning budget。一个重要结果是，在这些数学任务上，调大初始解题 budget 能达到相近 accuracy，而且 realized total model tokens 更少。因此 SeVRA 更适合需要显式验证、有限重试、答案修改审计的场景，而不是所有场景下的通用省算策略。CommonsenseQA 诊断显示动作选择具有任务依赖性：always-on verification 会伤害性能，self-consistency 只有在明显更高 token 成本下才有收益。廉价 feature gate 的表现接近 0.6B 和 1.7B QLoRA gate，在可接受小幅 accuracy gap 时更适合部署。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。重点看 Problem Formulation 中 helpful fix、harmful flip、realized total model tokens 的定义；看 Method 中 active verification prompt 和 gate label 的构造；看实验部分的预算匹配表，而不只看 MATH500 accuracy。最值得关注的是它如何把“验证是否有用”从样本难度中拆出来，以及为什么必须和调大初始 token budget 做公平比较。Appendix A 的 intervention screening 和 Appendix B 的 prompt 也值得扫读，因为它们决定了 selective policy 的上限。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This work frames extra reasoning as a serving-time allocation problem: after a frozen solver produces an initial answer, the system must decide whether to accept it or spend another call on active verification.","importance":"The paper is not a pretraining paper, but it is useful for understanding the interface between reasoning models, verifiers, and test-time compute. Its main lesson is that more reasoning is not uniformly beneficial. A second pass can repair failures, waste compute on already correct answers, or flip a correct answer into an incorrect one. This distinction is relevant for post-training data design as well: useful supervision should separate helpful fixes from harmful revisions, rather than simply rewarding longer traces or more self-correction attempts.","technical_approach":"SeVRA keeps the solver and intervention prompts frozen. A Qwen3-4B reasoning model first generates a base solution and answer. A lightweight gate then predicts whether active verification is likely to help, using only the problem, the base attempt, and serving-visible metadata such as completion status, token counts, finalizer use, estimated difficulty, verification need, and constraint density. Active verification asks the same frozen model to construct and execute at least two candidate-specific checks before changing the answer. These checks can reconstruct equations, test units and bounds, substitute the candidate answer, or solve through an independent route. The gate is trained offline with labels derived from intervention outcomes: whether verification repaired an incorrect answer or damaged a correct one. The paper compares a cheap logistic feature gate with Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B 4-bit QLoRA sequence-classifier gates.","experiments":"With a frozen Qwen3-4B solver, SeVRA reaches 76.3% accuracy on MATH500, compared with 75.5% for always verifying, while reducing post-generation tokens by 26.8% and lowering harmful flips. In frozen transfer to GSM8K, it verifies only a small fraction of examples, improves over the short-base solver, and reports no observed harmful flips. The budget-matched comparisons are the most important part: accepting the base answer, continuing, active verification, always verifying, and increasing the initial reasoning budget are all compared under realized token usage. On the tested math benchmarks, a longer initial solve reaches a similar accuracy region with fewer total realized model tokens. A CommonsenseQA diagnostic shows that the best action depends on the workload: always-on verification hurts, while self-consistency helps only at much higher token cost. The cheap feature gate nearly matches the larger QLoRA gates, making it the more practical option when the small accuracy gap is acceptable.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the problem formulation, especially the definitions of helpful fix, harmful flip, intervention rate, and realized total model tokens. Then read the method section for the active-verification prompt and the gate-label construction. In the experiments, prioritize the budget-matched tables over the headline accuracy numbers. Appendix A is useful for understanding why active verification was selected over continuation or critique-and-repair, and Appendix B is worth checking because the prompt defines the intervention being allocated.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"test-time compute分配文章，结论很值得注意：加verifier门控不如直接把初始CoT拉长，做budgeted reasoning的人看§4与Table 3即可。","en":"A test-time compute allocation paper whose punchline is that gated verification underperforms simply lengthening the initial CoT; read §4 and Table 3 if you work on budgeted reasoning."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Sajib Acharjee Dip","Dawei Zhou","Liqing Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["test-time-scaling","compute-allocation","verification","serving","reasoning-budget"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19808","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.19808","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}}]},{"id":"architecture","emoji":"🏗️","name":"LLM 架构与建模","description":"LLM 架构、MoE/SSM/混合架构、注意力结构、Diffusion/Flow/Masked/Any-order LM 等语言建模范式","papers":[{"id":"2606.19349","title":"Where to Place the Query? Unveiling and Mitigating Positional Bias in In-Context Learning for Diffusion LLMs via Decoding Dynamics","abstract":"While In-Context Learning (ICL) is extensively studied in Autoregressive (AR) LLMs, its mechanism within Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) remains largely unexplored. Unlike AR models restricted by unidirectional causal masking, dLLMs intrinsically utilize bidirectional attention, offering extensive spatial flexibility for query placement. Unfortunately, current practices conventionally inherit AR-style trailing-query templates, often overlooking the structural paradigm shift. diffusion LLM 做 ICL 时一个此前几乎被忽略的问题：query 应该插在 demonstrations 的什么位置。AR 模型里 query 基本只能放在末尾，但 dLLM 具备双向注意力，query 的空间位置变成了可配置变量；作者要回答的是，这个变量到底有多重要、为什么重要、以及能否在无标签条件下自动选择位置。","importance":"对预训练与新型语言建模范式的启发在于，它揭示了 diffusion LM 的 ICL 机制并不只是把 AR prompt 模板平移过来。双向建模带来的不是单纯“更灵活”，而是新的位置偏置和新的解码动力学。这个观察对 dLLM 的 prompt 设计、评测协议、甚至训练目标都有影响：如果 query placement 会显著改变性能，那么很多现有 dLLM ICL 结果可能混入了模板偏差，而不是纯模型能力差异。","technical_approach":"方法上分三步。第一，做严格控制实验，把 demonstrations 的内容和顺序固定，只枚举 query 插入位置 p∈{0,…,N}，从而把性能变化归因到空间位置本身。第二，做机制分析。空间上用 Attention Rollout 观察 query 是否更依赖邻近 examples，作者据此提出 dLLM 中存在明显 recency effect；时间上分析 decoding trajectory，发现 trailing query 更接近 AR 式左到右生成，而 prefix query 会诱发 boundary-first、更加不规则的迭代解码。第三，提出 Auto-ICL。作者认为 AR 常用的单步置信度不适合 dLLM，因为 diffusion 解码是多步演化过程，于是改用 trajectory-level 的 Average Confidence，对所有 decoding steps 的激活概率做聚合，用它在无监督条件下选择 query placement。论文还把 Vanilla、Prefix、Random 三种空间配置明确形式化，作为 dLLM ICL 的基础设定。","experiments":"实验使用 LLaDA-8B-Base 和 Dream-7B-Base，在 GSM8K、MATH、MBPP、Sudoku、Countdown 上做 4-shot 为主的评测。最关键的结果有两点。第一，位置带来的性能波动很大，作者用方差影响比 r=1.236 说明：仅仅改变 query 位置，带来的影响量级可以和替换 demonstrations 语义内容相当。第二，最优位置强烈依赖任务类型：GSM8K 这类 sequential reasoning 更偏好传统 trailing placement，而 Sudoku 这类 global-perception 任务在 prefix boundary 达到最好表现。Auto-ICL 方面，论文声称 Average Confidence 与生成稳定性高度相关，能在无标签条件下接近 oracle placement。对 skim 阅读者，最该记住的是：dLLM 的 ICL 里，query position 不是模板细节，而是一级超参数。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction、Problem Setup、External Sensitivity Experiments，再看机制分析和 Auto-ICL。预计 20–30 分钟能抓住主结论。若只看图表，优先看不同位置下的准确率矩阵、Attention Rollout 可视化、以及 Average Confidence 与真实效果相关性的结果。方法不复杂，价值主要在问题定义和现象拆解。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a neglected variable in in-context learning for diffusion LLMs: where the query should be inserted among the demonstrations. In autoregressive models the query is effectively forced to the end, but bidirectional diffusion LLMs allow flexible placement. The paper asks how important this choice is, why it matters, and whether the model can choose the placement automatically without labels.","importance":"The main relevance is conceptual. It shows that ICL in diffusion LLMs should not inherit AR prompting conventions by default. Bidirectional modeling introduces a new positional degree of freedom, and that degree of freedom materially affects performance. This has implications for prompt design, evaluation protocol, and possibly training objectives for diffusion-based language models, because part of the observed performance variance may come from template topology rather than model quality alone.","technical_approach":"The study proceeds in three stages. First, it uses a controlled setup where the demonstration set and its ordering are fixed while only the query insertion position is varied over all possible slots. This isolates spatial position as the causal variable. Second, it analyzes the mechanism behind the effect. Spatially, Attention Rollout is used to show a strong recency effect: the query tends to anchor on nearby examples. Temporally, the paper examines decoding trajectories and finds that a trailing query induces a more AR-like left-to-right generation pattern, whereas a prefix query leads to boundary-first and more irregular iterative decoding. Third, it proposes Auto-ICL, an unsupervised routing method. Instead of using single-step confidence, which is natural in AR decoding but mismatched to iterative diffusion decoding, the method uses Average Confidence aggregated across all decoding steps as a trajectory-level proxy for generation stability.","experiments":"Experiments are run on LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base across GSM8K, MATH, MBPP, Sudoku, and Countdown, mainly in a 4-shot setting. Two findings stand out. First, query position has a large effect: the variance induced purely by moving the query is reported to be on par with changing the semantic content of the demonstrations, with a variance impact ratio of r = 1.236. Second, the best placement is task-dependent. Sequential reasoning tasks such as GSM8K prefer the standard trailing query, while global-perception tasks such as Sudoku perform best with prefix placement. The proposed Auto-ICL uses Average Confidence to select placements without labels and is reported to approach oracle routing across tasks.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, focus on the introduction, the problem setup, the external sensitivity experiments, and then the Auto-ICL section. Around 20 to 30 minutes should be enough to extract the main message. If you only inspect figures, prioritize the accuracy-by-position matrices, the Attention Rollout visualizations, and the plots showing the correlation between Average Confidence and actual performance. f","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T11:14:38Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_20097.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是长上下文模型中 data、是否依赖特定检索模板。方法部分重点看 head-wise scale-normalized fusion，因为 FA 和 LA 在表示形态上的差异是混合架构训练不稳的主要来源。实验部分建议看三个比较：不同混合粒度的取舍、FA heads 压缩比例的消融、15B tokens 长上下文迁移结果。若关注预训练成本，最值得细读的是三阶段 transfer pipeline。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses how to combine full attention and linear attention for long-context language models. Existing hybrids often operate at the layer level, but layers are a coarse unit. The authors show that attention heads within the same layer are functionally heterogeneous, and that precise long-context retrieval depends on a sparse subset of heads. This motivates using the head, rather than the layer, as the unit of attention hybridization.","importance":"The work is relevant to base-model architecture and continual pretraining. It turns an interpretability signal into an architecture conversion rule: identify retrieval-critical heads in a pretrained full-attention model, keep full attention only for those heads, and convert the rest to linear attention. This gives a practical path to long-context adaptation with limited additional training tokens while preserving general reasoning better than coarse layer replacement. The contribution is mainly an architecture and transfer recipe, not a new language modeling objective.","technical_approach":"HydraHead performs head-level attention hybridization. The selection step computes per-head logit contributions on long-context retrieval examples to find heads that materially support the correct answer token. These heads are sparse and tend to appear in deeper layers. By contrast, layer output similarity forms a smooth block-like pattern, which gives little signal for deciding which entire layers should use full attention. HydraHead keeps full attention for the selected retrieval-critical heads and assigns linear attention to the remaining heads. Since full attention and linear attention produce features with different scales and structures, the method adds head-wise scale-normalized fusion to reduce interference. Training follows a three-stage transfer pipeline: parameter inheritance from the pretrained checkpoint with layer-wise alignment, global distillation, and long-context fine-tuning.","experiments":"The experiments are built on Qwen3-1.7B under a unified training setup, comparing layer-wise, token-wise, and head-wise hybrid strategies. The reported pattern is clear: layer-wise hybrids extrapolate better in length but lose more general reasoning ability, while token-wise and ordinary head-wise variants preserve reasoning better but are weaker on long-context extrapolation. HydraHead improves this trade-off. It keeps more than a 10% advantage over layer-wise baselines on hard reasoning tasks, and with an aggressive 7:1 linear-attention to full-attention ratio it matches the overall performance of a 3:1 layer-wise hybrid. With only 15B training tokens, it improves NIAH performance at 512k context by more than 69% over the pretrained baseline, while maintaining general reasoning performance.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take 30 to 40 minutes. Start with Figure 2, because it provides the main evidence for moving from layer-level to head-level hybridization. Then read the head-selection procedure and check how per-head logit contribution is defined, how much calibration data is required, and whether the signal depends on the retrieval template. In the method section, focus on head-wise scale-normalized fusion, since mixing full and linear attention is mainly difficult because their output features are not naturally aligned. In the experiments, prioritize three parts: the comparison across hybridization granularities, the ablation over the full-attention head budget, and the 15B-token long-context transfer result. For pretraining researchers, the three-stage transfer pipeline is the most reusable component.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇值得看，因为它把 hybrid attention 的设计粒度从 layer 改到 head，确实可能改变你做长上下文架构搜索的 prior；先看分析图和 ablation，别先信总分。","en":"Worth reading because it shifts hybrid attention design from the layer level to the head level, which could genuinely change your long-context architecture prior; start with the analysis and ablations, not the headline metrics."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhentao Tan","Wei Chen","Jingyi Shen","Yao Liu","Xu Shen","Yue Wu","Jieping Ye"],"notable_authors":["Jieping Ye"],"tags":["hybrid-attention","long-context","linear-attention","interpretability","architecture"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20097","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.20097","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.19538","title":"ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence","abstract":"Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T06:57:31Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19849.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是流式 压缩等局部模块，但没有回答一个更关键的问题：整个系统能否长期稳定地“边看边处理”，并在任意长视频上维持固定延迟和内存预算。ViCoStream 的核心问题因此是，如何把流式 VideoLLM 视为一个多阶段协调流水线，识别并约束最慢阶段，使吞吐和查询延迟都可控，而不是让瓶颈在不同模块之间来回转移。","importance":"它对基座研究的直接相关性不如预训练方法论文强，但对大模型系统和长上下文多模态 serving 有现实启发。价值主要在于两点。第一，它把 streaming inference 明确建模成 stage-wise pipeline，强调系统吞吐由最慢阶段决定，这比单独报告某个模块加速更接近真实部署。第二，它提出“有界视觉上下文 + 查询侧检索”的组合，让每个 chunk 的 LLM prefill 成本不再随视频历史线性增长。这种把上下文成本做成常数上界的思路，对长上下文多模态模型、在线 agent 感知、以及训练后部署阶段的系统设计都有参考意义。若从预训练研究者视角看，这篇更像 serving/系统论文，但它清楚展示了多模态 token 密度、上下文管理和推理延迟之间的耦合关系。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是把连续视频流处理拆成四个顺序阶段：视觉预处理、视觉编码、token dropping、LLM inference，然后通过 chunk 化和并行调度把不同 chunk 放到不同阶段同时执行。作者不是简单做异步，而是围绕“最慢阶段决定可持续 FPS”这个约束来设计整套系统。\n\n第一步是 chunk-wise execution。视频帧不按单帧处理，而是按固定 chunk size c 分块。这样做的原因很直接：ViT 在小批量并行下效率明显高于逐帧串行，能更好利用 GPU。第二步是 CUDA stream overlap，把预处理和编码等前端操作重叠执行，减少 GPU 空转。第三步是 projector 侧的 chunk-level temporal token dropping，在视觉特征投到语言空间后丢弃冗余 token，直接控制后续 LLM 的计算量和显存占用。第四步是 LLM 侧的 bounded visual attention 加 query-side retrieval：模型不会在每次查询时看到全部历史视觉 token，而是只保留一个受限的可见视觉上下文窗口，同时通过检索机制访问与当前问题相关的过去观测。\n\n这套设计的关键不是某个单独技巧，而是几个约束一起工作。chunking 提高前端吞吐，token dropping 控制 token 密度，bounded attention 把 prefill 成本封顶，retrieval 则尽量补偿截断历史带来的信息损失。作者把这件事表述为 slowest-stage scheduling problem：只要每个 chunk 在最慢阶段的处理时间低于视频到达间隔，系统就能持续实时运行。这样，查询时的 TTFT 也不再随着视频总时长增长，而是主要由受限上下文下的一次 prefill 决定。\n\n和已有 streaming VideoLLM 的区别也比较清楚。delayed streaming 把大部分计算拖到用户提问时做一次大 prefill，延迟和显存都随历史增长；已有 continuous streaming 工作虽然做了稀疏编码、关键帧选择或 cache 压缩，但常缺少端到端流水线视角，容易出现“前面快了，后面堵住”的瓶颈迁移。ViCoStream 试图把这些局部优化放到统一调度框架里讨论。","experiments":"实验主结果是系统吞吐和精度折中。论文报告，在 30% token retention 下，ViCoStream 可达到 134 FPS，同时多数中等压缩设置下精度与 full-history baseline 基本持平。这个结果说明，只要视觉 token 密度和可见历史被合理约束，流式 VideoLLM 的可持续吞吐可以远高于常见实时视频帧率，而不必把全部历史都塞进 LLM。\n\n更有价值的是系统性分析，而不是单个 headline 数字。作者考察了 chunk granularity、token retention ratio、visual-context size、retrieval scope 等因素如何影响瓶颈迁移。这里的重点不是“某个参数最优”，而是不同设置会把瓶颈从视觉前端转移到 LLM prefill，或者反过来。比如 token dropping 虽然减少了 LLM 计算，但如果视觉编码仍是最慢阶段，整体 FPS 不一定提升；反过来，如果前端已经足够快，而 visual context 不受限，TTFT 仍会随历史增长。论文想证明的是，只有把各阶段一起协调，才能得到稳定的吞吐-延迟曲线。\n\n从阅读价值看，建议特别关注作者是否给出了长时流上的内存曲线和 TTFT 曲线。如果这些曲线确实随 stream length 保持近似平坦，那就说明 bounded visual attention 的设计是有效的；如果只是短视频上快一些，意义会小很多。摘要里没有列出完整 benchmark 名称和每项精度数字，但全文的实验组织方式明显偏系统论文：强调 sustained FPS、TTFT、memory budget 和 accuracy 的联合分析。","reading_guide":"这篇适合带着系统问题去读。先看 Introduction 和 pipeline 的设计，这部分决定了“延迟不随历史增长”是否成立。\n\n实验部分建议优先看四类图表：各 stage latency breakdown、FPS 随 token retention 或 chunk size 变化的曲线、TTFT/显存随 stream length 变化的曲线、以及 accuracy-efficiency trade-off 表。若只想判断是否值得跟进，20 到 30 a systems problem in streaming VideoLLMs rather than a single-module acceleration trick. In deployment, a streaming model must satisfy two constraints at once: it must ingest video fast enough to keep up with the incoming stream, and it must answer user queries with low time-to-first-token. Prior work often speeds up one component at a time, such as visual encoding, token pruning, or cache compression, but that does not tell you whether the full system can sustain real-time operation over long streams. ViCoStream asks how to treat streaming VideoLLM inference as a coordinated multi-stage pipeline, identify the true bottleneck, and keep both throughput and query latency bounded as video history grows.","importance":"This is more of a serving/systems paper than a pretraining paper, but it is still relevant to foundation-model researchers working on multimodal deployment, long-context inference, and token-budget management. Its main contribution is conceptual: streaming inference is framed as a stage-wise pipeline whose sustainable FPS is determined by the slowest stage, not by isolated module speedups. The second useful idea is the combination of bounded visual context and query-side retrieval, which keeps per-chunk LLM prefill cost from growing linearly with total stream length. Generating one image requires thousands of serial forward passes, leading to a severe memory bandwidth bottleneck (memory wall). Existing speculative decoding methods migrated from NLP are constrained by the 1D sequential assumption, resulting in low draft acceptance rates and limited speedups for visual generation.","importance":"It provides a plug-and-play inference acceleration scheme for unified autoregressive multimodal models, requiring no modification to pretrained backbones. It also clarifies a key tradeoff in multimodal unified modeling: while 1D sequence formulation enables shared modeling across modalities during pretraining, modality-specific geometric structure can be leveraged at inference to recover efficiency without breaking pretrained capabilities. This logic can also inform the design of multimodal token prediction strategies.","technical_approach":"Spatially Speculative Decoding (SSD) breaks from 1D sequential drafting. It exploits the fact that, in raster-scan order, a token’s vertical neighbor (directly below) has predictive dependency comparable to its immediate horizontal neighbor, so it drafts entire rows of candidate tokens in parallel across horizontal and vertical axes. Lightweight prediction heads are attached to the continuous hidden features before the final RMSNorm of the pretrained backbone, rather than predicting discrete visual tokens directly — discrete visual tokens have flat probability distributions with unstable learning signals, while continuous features provide more stable supervision. During verification, strict binary rejection is replaced with soft correction: the backbone’s forward pass output auto-fixes minor representational mismatches, and unresolved discrepancies can be handled with additional forward passes. The entire module is fully compatible with existing pretrained autoregressive visual backbones.","experiments":"Evaluated on three widely adopted high-performance autoregressive visual/multimodal models: 12.19× wall-clock speedup on Lumina-mGPT, 5.74× on Janus-Pro, and up to 13.28× on Emu3. Acceptance rate measurements confirm that the vertical +1 position has 30.32% acceptance, close to the 43.27% of the horizontal +1 position, validating the 2D spatial dependency assumption. The drafting heads introduce negligible FLOPs overhead, even when generating 240 draft tokens per step.","reading_guide":"Start with Figures 1 and 2 to grasp the core intuition of 2D spatial dependency and decoding flow. We introdu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T17:49:36Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_20537.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在单流/小 batch、端侧 GPU 的“物理 AI/交互式”低延迟推理场景里，主流 serving（vLLM PagedAttention、SGLang RadixAttention）围绕高并发吞吐与 KV 复用设计，其 CUDA Graph 捕获刻意不把 KV 绑定成可冻结的自包含状态，导致难以在中断、分支、回到旧会话等交互变化下快速恢复一个完整、可继续执行的推理状态。","importance":"这是推理系统论文，不是预训练方法论文；对基座预训练的直接影响较弱。价值主要在工程接口层：如果你的研究需要在端侧/机器人/语音交互里做“频繁打断与回滚”的在线数据采集或闭环训练，执行状态的可 checkpoint/restore 会显著影响实验吞吐与可重复性。它也提醒一个常被忽略的点：KV cache 只是执行状态的一部分，面向交互控制的 serving 需要更显式的状态边界与生命周期管理。","technical_approach":"论文提出两层系统：\n1) FlashRT：一个 latency-first 的白盒 runtime，用来把 concurrency=1 的冷启动路径做得足够便宜（计入 graph replay 与状态准备成本，不含 tokenizer/网络）。\n2) Execution-State Capsule：把“执行状态”做成图绑定（graph-bound）的自包含胶囊，用于 checkpoint 与 restore。动机是：vLLM/SGLang 的 CUDA Graph 通过可变 block-table 间接读取 KV，从而支持任意物理块复用；但这种间接性意味着“捕获的 graph + 绑定 buffer”不是一个可冻结快照，无法直接拿来做会话级恢复。\n该工作转而面向相反目标：在小并发下，牺牲部分通用的块复用灵活性，换取可冻结、可搬运、可快速恢复的完整 continuation state，以支持新 turn、分支、interrupt、re-entry 等交互控制流。","experiments":"你提供的全文片段仅覆盖引言与问题设定，没有性能表、端到端延迟分解、与 vLLM/SGLang 的对比数字、硬件型号、模型规模、以及 capsule 的内存开销等关键信息，因此无法给出具体实验结论与量化收益。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 15–25 分钟）：\n- 先读 Introduction，把它的目标函数记清楚：只优化 concurrency=1 的 wall-clock latency，并且强调“可恢复的执行状态”而不只是 KV 复用。\n- 重点看 §2（runtime 的执行路径、graph capture/replay 的边界）与 §3（capsule 的状态组成、绑定方式、restore 流程）。\n- 看实验时优先找三类图表：① 端到端延迟分解（prefill/decoding/状态准备/graph replay）；② 中断/分支/回滚场景的恢复时间；③ capsule 的显存占用与可并发会话数上限。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"For extreme low-latency, small-batch (often single-stream) on-device serving in interactive/physical-AI loops, mainstream KV-cache systems (vLLM PagedAttention, SGLang RadixAttention) are optimized for high-concurrency throughput and capture CUDA Graphs without binding KV as a self-contained snapshot. That design hinders fast checkpoint/restore of a complete continuation state under interrupts, branching, and session re-entry.","importance":"Primarily an inference/serving systems contribution rather than pretraining. The practical relevance to foundation-model research shows up when experiments depend on tight interactive loops (robotics, voice, coding assistants) where frequent interruption/rollback affects data collection and iteration speed. bottleneck in attention-distillation KL. Standard implementations materialize two full attention distributions before reducing their KL divergence, which costs O(N_Q N_K) extra memory and traffic. At 64K context with 32 heads in BF16, storing the two distributions alone is about 512 GB, making long-context attention distillation impractical on a single accelerator.","importance":"The work matters for long-context foundation-model training, especially sparse-attention training. Recent sparse-attention systems often distill an indexer or sparse attention pattern from dense attention. If the KL loss itself materializes quadratic attention maps, the distillation objective becomes a system bottleneck. StreamKL turns this loss into a fused streaming primitive, making attention distillation more compatible with long-context training recipes.","technical_approach":"StreamKL derives an online formulation for KL divergence between two attention distributions. Unlike ordinary attention, the loss couples two independent softmaxes and a weighted logit-difference term, so the accumulated quantity must be rescaled correctly when the running maxima and log-sum-exp values of both distributions change. The forward kernel streams key tiles through on-chip SRAM and accumulates the row-wise KL without writing either attention matrix to HBM. The backward pass saves log-sum-exp vectors and recomputes attention probabilities tile by tile, avoiding quadratic intermediates. The implementation includes split-K forward kernels for small-query workloads such as autoregressive decoding, fused backward kernels that reduce redundant HBM traffic and logit recomputation, and Hopper-specific TMA-based data movement.","experiments":"Evaluation is reported on NVIDIA H200 and A100 GPUs. In the backward pass, it reports up to 6.5× speedup over PyTorch for non-causal inputs and 14.0× under causal masking. The extra HBM footprint for the distillation loss is reduced from O(N_Q N_K) to O(1), enabling 64K+ context settings on a single GPU where materialization-based baselines run out of memory.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes around 25–35 minutes. 基线，要么需要端到端优化，代价高、工程复杂。AIR 要解决的核心点，是在保持 layer-local、闭式、低成本的前提下，把逐元素的后向影响信号真正整合进低秩近似，而不是只把 backward signal 当作一个粗糙权重或额外启发式。","importance":"对大模型研究的直接意义不在预训练本身，而在于它把“函数保持型压缩”往前推进了一步：一方面说明 activation-aware 已经不够，压缩时需要区分哪些残差会传到输出端；另一方面也说明 backward information 并不一定需要 critic-like 的全局优化或复杂蒸馏，局部闭式更新也能吃到这部分收益。对基座研究员更有启发的是方法论：很多压缩、剪枝、量化问题里，前向统计和后向影响往往都重要，关键不是有没有 backward signal，而是如何把它嵌入一个可解、稳定、可扩展的目标。AIR 给出的答案是把 influence 作为逐元素加权残差引入，再用 rank-wise ALS 近似求解。这种设计对后续做 low-rank adaptation、结构化压缩、甚至训练期正则化都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"实验主线比较清楚。核心结果是在 LLaMA-7B 上，不同保留率下 AIR 相比 SVD-LLM(W) 的 WikiText-2 perplexity 明显更低：在 60% / 40% / 20% 参数保留率时，困惑度分别降低约 18% / 33% / 45%。这说明压缩越激进，逐元素 influence weighting 的收益越大，因为误差预算更紧，残差该放在哪些位置变得更重要。\n\n数据效率也是一个亮点。作者报告 AIR 在大致相同质量下只需要约 10% 的 calibration data，就能匹配 SVD-LLM(W) 的效果，也就是校准数据需求下降约 90%。这点很实用，因为很多后训练压缩流程真正卡的不是算法，而是拿不到足够代表性的校准集。\n\n泛化方面，方法不只在 LLaMA-7B 上有效，也覆盖了 LLaMA、Mistral、Vicuna 等家族。说明它并不依赖某个特定模型的奇异值谱形态。消融里有几个值得注意的结论：第一，合理的 backward signal 之间差异很小，LRP-epsilon、Weight×Gradient、Fisher 在 AIR 框架下几乎等价；第二，若换成无信息或弱信息的 influence baseline，性能会明显掉下去，说明 backward signal 仍然要有基本质量；第三，all-ones anchor 和 delta 的设置会影响稳定性，纯 influence-only 的加权不如 activation + influence 的混合目标稳。\n\n系统指标上，作者给了部署层面的数字：在 40GB A100 上，60% 参数保留率可带来约 64% 的 peak memory 和 53% 的 per-token latency，相当于参数节省确实转化成了实际推理收益，而不是只停留在理论压缩率。另一个重要结果是 AIR + LoRA 在各压缩率下都优于 ACIP，说明 layer-local 压缩和轻量端到端补偿是互补的。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议抓三部分。\n\n第一，看方法部分里 hybrid objective 的定义，以及为什么 element-wise weighted low-rank approximation 不能直接用标准 SVD 解。这里是整篇文章最核心的数学动机。若只看结论，很容易误以为 AIR 只是“给 SVD 加了个 backward 权重”，但真正有效的是后面的 rank-wise ALS 近似求解。\n\n第二，看 ALS 更新的推导和更新顺序。重点不是把公式逐行抄下来，而是理解两个设计：为什么从 SVD-LLM(W) 的 activation-aware optimum 出发；为什么按 rank 从后往前扫一遍就够。这里能看出作者在效果、复杂度、稳定性之间的平衡。\n\n第三，看消融表和 Figure 3 一类分析图。建议重点确认三件事：不同 influence signal 是否真的接近等效；all-ones anchor 是否必要；校准数据量缩小时 AIR 的退化曲线是否更缓。若只想快速判断是否值得复现，这几张图比主表更有信息量。\n\n这篇适合 30 到 45 分钟泛读。若你做的是压缩、低秩适配、训练后结构化优化，可以再花时间看附录里关于 LRP 和 weighted objective 的推导。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"AIR addresses a specific weakness of SVD-based LLM compression. Plain SVD is cheap and structurally aligned with transformer linear layers, but it only minimizes matrix reconstruction error. Activation-aware variants such as SVD-LLM(W) preserve forward activations better, yet they still do not distinguish which approximation errors actually matter for the final prediction. Prior backward-signal methods either underperform activation-only baselines or rely on expensive end-to-end optimization. 之间，并且随底层模型能力增强而上升。这个趋势说明系统设计不是在“制造幻觉信号”，而是在放大模型已有但不稳定的审阅能力。\n\n扰动 benchmark 上，最强配置同样是 OpenAIReview + GPT-5.5，能召回 71.6% 的注入错误。按错误类型看，OpenAIReview 相对 zero-shot baseline 的优势主要出现在 prose-level 错误，而不是局部数学 However, this relationship c","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T19:59:26Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19558.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"量化模型部署时，实践中常用相对 BF16 参考模型的 KLD、PPL 等 fidelity metrics 来低成本筛选量化版本。该研究检验了一个更具体的问题：这些指标是否能在“接近原模型质量”的候选量化模型之间提供可靠排序，而不只是识别已经明显退化的模型。","importance":"对 LLM 部署评估和压缩 recipe 选择有直接启发。结论是：KLD/PPL 可以作为粗粒度退化检测器，但不能替代 near-baseline 区间的任务评测。这个区间被称为 silent zone：模型输出分布确实偏离了参考模型，但偏离幅度不再对应下游质量排序。对基座研究员的价值在于，它提醒量化、蒸馏、低精度训练等工作不能只看 distributional fidelity；质量变化还取决于偏离方向，即这些差异是把错误改对，还是把正确改错。","technical_approach":"研究构造了两个量化模型队列：28 个 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 的 GGUF 量化版本，以及 41 个 Devstral-Small-2-24B-Instruct-2512 的量化版本。前者是 MoE，后者是 dense code-specialized 模型，用来避免结论只依赖单一架构。fidelity 主要计算 KL(p_BF16 || p_quant) 的 next-token 分布差异，并比较 PPL ratio、Δlog-PPL、top-1 agreement、response-only、长上下文、on-task calibration 等 14 类变体。下游质量用 HumanEval、LiveCodeBench v6、IFEval、BFCL v3、GSM8K、GSM8K-v、MMLU、MATH-500 等任务的归一化均值表示。核心分析不是只做相关性，而是把得分差分解成 volume 和 direction：volume 表示量化模型与参考模型在样本正确性上发生翻转的数量，direction 表示这些翻转中 leapfrog 和 drop 的比例。公式为 score = ref_score + vol · (2f - 1) / N。KLD 更稳定地追踪 volume，但下游分数还取决于 direction；silent zone 中 direction 信号弱，因而 KLD 失去排序能力。","experiments":"全量候选集合上，KLD 与综合 benchmark 分数有较强负相关：Qwen 队列 Spearman ρ = -0.72，Devstral 队列 ρ = -0.86，二者 p < 0.001。这说明 KLD 能识别明显损坏的低质量量化版本。但把范围限制到 near-baseline 候选后，相关性消失：Qwen ρ = +0.00，Devstral ρ = -0.24 且 p = 0.36。这个 collapse 在 14 种 fidelity metric 变体中都存在，silent-zone 内 |ρ| ≤ 0.25，且 p > 0.10。per-prompt 层面也类似：在 LiveCodeBench 上，KLD 区分同一模型 pass 与 fail 的能力很弱，fail-vs-pass 几何均值比值仅为 1.08 到 1.22；用 per-prompt KLD 在两个量化模型分歧时选择更好输出，错误率为 50.6% 到 57.7%，接近随机。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中 silent zone / lossy zone 的定义，Methodology 中两个模型队列和 benchmark 设计，Table 2 的分区相关性，Table 3 的 14 种 metric 变体，Table 5 的 volume-direction 分解。若关心量化部署，per-prompt routing 分析值得细看；它说明 token-level fidelity 很难直接转化成在线选择或动态路由信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The study asks whether cheap fidelity metrics, especially KL divergence or perplexity against a BF16 reference model, can reliably rank quantized LLMs that are already close to baseline quality. The target use case is practical model selection among many public quantization variants, not detecting obviously broken models.","importance":"The main lesson is operationally useful for compression and deployment work: KLD and PPL are coarse degradation detectors, but they are poor ranking signals inside the near-baseline region. This matters for LLM pretraining and systems researchers because distributional closeness is often used as a proxy in quantization, distillation, and low-precision validation. The results show that magnitude of displacement is not enough; downstream quality depends on whether the changed decisions are helpful or harmful.","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"Across the full cohorts, KLD correlates strongly with benchmark quality: Spearman ρ = -0.72 for Qwen and ρ = -0.86 for Devstral, both with p < 0.001. reasoning model 的训练信号设计有启发。它说明形式系统的 verifier 不只是最终裁判，也可以提供中间监督。这个观察可以迁移到代码、数学、工具调用和 RL 后训练：如果只用二值 reward，会丢掉大量可学习信息；如果把错误类型、局部进展、失败轨迹显式建模，搜索和训练都会更稳定。贡献量级偏方法集成和实验验证，不是新的语言建模范式。","technical_approach":"VERITAS 使用四个角色共享同一个 proof state：Strategist 先选择高层证明策略，如 direct、induction、contradiction、cases、rewrite、apply_lemma；Tactician 用 Claude Sonnet 4.6 生成 tactic，输入包括当前 goal、Mathlib 检索结果、策略、critic 建议，以及此前失败 tactic 和 Lean 错误信息；Critic 用 Claude Haiku 4.5 给部分证明状态打分，并给出下一步建议和剪枝意见；Retriever 返回 top-5 Mathlib premises。核心设计不是多 agent 本身，而是所有角色都看见 Lean 的四类信号：syntax validity、type correctness、goal progress、proof completion。搜索采用两阶段协议：第一阶段做 Best-of-N 平铺采样；第二阶段只对第一阶段失败的定理运行 critic-guided MCTS。这样可以保证同一次 VERITAS run 内，第一阶段已经解出的题不会被第二阶段覆盖掉，第二阶段新增解题可以归因于 verifier feedback 引导的搜索。论文还加入 batched Lean validation，把每次 expansion 的验证调用从 O(K) 降到 O(1)，降低了 Lean 检查开销。","experiments":"主要结果在两个 Lean 4 benchmark 上。miniF2F 上，VERITAS 解出 99/244，准确率 40.6%；独立运行的 Claude Best-of-5 是 36.9%，手工 Portfolio 是 26.2%。阶段分解显示有 11 道 miniF2F 题是 MCTS 阶段独有解，说明改进不只是采样随机性带来的重叠。VERITAS-CombiBench 是论文释放的 55 题组合数学 benchmark，作者强调这些题更依赖正确 Mathlib lemma 和多步修正。该 benchmark 上 Best-of-5 只有 1.8%，Portfolio 是 3.6%，VERITAS 达到 7.3%。一个有信息量的失败模式是：平铺采样容易编造 Mathlib lemma 名称，Lean 会立刻拒绝；VERITAS 把这些错误作为负例注入后续生成，能逐步修正 lemma 名称或调用方式。需要注意，绝对解题率仍然不高，且系统依赖闭源 Claude 模型，结论更适合作为 verifier-in-the-loop 设计证据，而不是证明该框架已经解决形式化推理。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 25 分钟。先看 Introduction 中关于“thin communication”的问题定义，这部分最能解释为什么二值反馈不够。然后看 The VERITAS Framework 里的 proof state 定义、四个 agent 分工和两阶段协议，重点关注失败 tactic 如何作为 negative examples 回到 Tactician。实验部分优先看 miniF2F 的 phase-wise attribution、CombiBench 对比，以及 failure-mode taxonomy。可以略读 prompt 细节和工程性配置。若关心 RL/post-train，可重点思考 Lean 的结构化错误如何转成训练时的 dense feedback，而不是只看 MCTS 解题率。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"VERITAS addresses a narrow but important bottleneck in LLM-based formal proving: verifier feedback is usually collapsed into a binary accept/reject signal. Lean often returns much richer information, including parse errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress, and completion status. The paper asks whether routing these signals back into generation and search can improve zero-shot proof search beyond flat sampling.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to researchers studying reasoning models and training-signal quality. It treats the verifier as a source of intermediate supervision rather than only a final judge. That design point carries over to code, math, tool use, and RL-style post-training: binary rewards discard useful structure, while typed failures and partial progress can guide both inference-time search and future training objectives. The contribution is mainly a verifier-in-the-loop search protocol with empirical attribution, not a new language-modeling objective.","technical_approach":"$1f","experiments":"On miniF2F, VERITAS solves 99 out of 244 problems, or 40.6%, compared with an independently run Claude Best-of-5 baseline at 36.9% and a handcrafted portfolio at 26.2%. The phase-wise analysis reports 11 miniF2F theorems solved only by the MCTS phase. On the released VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem Lean 4 combinatorics benchmark, Best-of-5 reaches 1.8%, the portfolio reaches 3.6%, and VERITAS reaches 7.3%. The most useful qualitative result is the error pattern: unguided sampling often hallucinates Mathlib lemma names, while feedback-guided search can use Lean’s rejection messages as negative evidence and correct subsequent attempts. The absolute solve rates remain modest, and the setup depends on proprietary Claude models, so the evidence should be read as support for structured verifier feedback rather than as a general solution to formal theorem proving.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25 minutes. Prompt engineering and implementation details can be skimmed unless reproducing the system. 把评测点转到语义恢复和流程状态保持。这个区分很重要，因为模型可能已经学会停止发声，但仍会在恢复时复读、忽略纠正信息，或丢失任务进度。对训练语音大模型来说，这类失败更接近数据和目标函数缺口，而不是单纯的低延迟推理问题。","technical_approach":"IHBench 构造的是带状态机的多轮语音工作流。数据覆盖 10 个企业流程领域，包括 assessment，用类型相关 rubric 判断恢复质量。评测由 LLM judge 执行，并通过人类评审、一致性分析和外部 benchmark 对照来校验可靠性。","experiments":"论文评测了 27 个 audio-language model 配置，分析维度包括总体分数、不同 interruption 类型的表现、随对话深度增加的退化，以及 audio input 和 recover correctly after a user interruption in a structured workflow. The focus is not only whether the model stops speaking, but what it says next: whether it handles the interjection, updates relevant information, avoids repeating content the user already heard, and resumes at the correct workflow step.","importance":"This is primarily an evaluation paper, but it is useful for researchers working on speech LMs and native audio agents. Existing interruption benchmarks mostly measure barge-in, endpointing, and turn-taking behavior. IHBench shifts the measurement to semantic recovery and workflow-state maintenance after the interruption. That distinction matters because a model can stop speaking at the right time and still fail by re-reading the interrupted sentence, ignoring a correction, or losing its place in the task. For training audio-language models, these failures point to missing supervision and weak recovery objectives rather than only inference-latency problems.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"The paper evaluates 27 audio-language model configurations. u","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T21:36:09Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19613.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"StaminaBench 要解决的是编码 Agent 评测过于单轮化的问题：现有 SWE-Bench、TerminalBench 等主要看一次性任务是否完成，不能衡量 Agent 在几十到上百轮需求变更中维持代码一致性、理解历史约束、持续调试和管理上下文的能力。","importance":"这项工作对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对代码 Agent change，包括添加、删除、重命名实体，修改字段，增加关系和 analytics endpoint。每一轮 Agent 根据自然语言需求修改代码，系统用隐藏测试通过 HTTP 检查实现是否匹配当前参考状态。更一般地，作者把多轮 Agent benchmark 抽象成 reference system 和 agent system：参考系统有状态空间、初始状态分布、合法动作集合、确定性 transition function 和环境动作选择策略；Agent 需要在每一步追踪参考状态。动作序列可以由硬编码 sampler 或受结构化动作空间约束的 LLM-driven sampler 产生，但规格和测试不依赖 LLM 生成，从而降低不可复现和测试错误风险。","experiments":"实验覆盖 6 个开源 harness 和 7 个开源模型，模型规模从 24B 到 744B benchmark 如何保证有效性、可无限生成和可控难度。实验部分优先看不同 harness、是否提供 test feedback、是否允许 retry 的对比结果，这些比模型排名更有信息量。附录中 REST API 任务定义和 action space 适合快速扫一遍，用来判断这个基准是否覆盖真实代码维护中的复杂性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"StaminaBench targets a gap in coding-agent evaluation: most benchmarks measure whether an agent can solve one isolated task, while real agent-assisted programming often involves dozens or hundreds of consecutive change requests on an evolving codebase.","importance":"The paper is not a direct contribution to base-model pretraining, but it is useful for evaluating long-horizon coding agents. Its main value is the shift from single-task success to survival over an evolving project state. For model researchers, it highlights that long-context coding ability depends on state tracking, selective memory, accumulated error control, and the quality of the execution harness. It also shows that test feedback and retry loops can materially change measured capability, so benchmark results should not be read as model-only signals.","technical_approach":"The benchmark procedurally generates REST API server tasks. An episode starts with an initial API specification and then applies a sequence of schema changes: adding, deleting, or renaming entities, modifying fields, adding relationships, and adding analytics endpoints. The agent only sees feature descriptions and optionally textual test feedback. Hidden tests interact with the server through HTTP, so the implementation is treated as a black box and the evaluation is language-agnostic. The broader framework defines a reference system with a state space, an initial-state distribution, valid actions, a deterministic transition function, and an environment action-selection policy. The agent system must keep its code aligned with the reference trajectory. Change sequences can be sampled by a hardcoded policy or an LLM-driven sampler constrained by the structured action space, while specs and tests remain procedurally generated.","experiments":"The empirical study covers 6 open-source harnesses and 7 open-source models ranging from 24B to 744B total parameters. The benchmark runs up to 100 interaction turns, producing codebases of up to roughly 6,000 lines. The main result is that all tested systems fail early, even when using strong models. Harness quality is a prerequisite for good performance. Enabling test feedback and retry loops substantially improves results, indicating that many failures are tied to debugging signals and tool-loop design rather than model weights alone. The paper also studies different environment samplers for generating valid long-horizon change sequences.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. The same signal drives RL with verifiable rewards, math data curation, synthetic curricula, and verifier training. sampling seed solves in six tries are ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T22:31:10Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19636.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作诊断 pass@k 作为数学推理难度估计的盲区：一个题目在若干次温度采样中没有被解出，并不一定说明模型不会做，也可能只是采样轨迹没有到达可解区域。","importance":"这篇更接近预训练和 post-train 接口问题。pass@k 不只是评测指标，还被用于 RL with verifiable rewards、数学数据筛选、合成 curriculum、verifier 训练和难度分桶。如果 pass@k=0 的样本中有一部分其实可被同一个模型在非采样轴上解出，那么数据过滤、奖励训练和难度标注都会把“未被采样触达”误当成“真实困难”。这会影响 RLVR 数据保留策略、hard negative 构造、推理数据课程和 verifier 校准。论文的贡献不是提出一个实用解码器，而是给出一个诊断：采样多样性不能代表模型可达解空间的全部。","technical_approach":"作者比较两类等计算预算的探索方式。第一类是常规 temperature sampling，用 k=6 条 sampled chains 估计 pass@k，并把没有任何链到达正确答案的题目视为最难层。第二类是 deterministic regime：greedy decoding 加上 5 个通过 activation grafting 产生的确定性变体。activation grafting 的做法是在最后一个 prompt token 的 residual-stream hidden state 上替换为廉价构造的 synthetic vector，然后继续 greedy decoding。这样可以固定 prompt 和权重，只改变确定性前向轨迹。它不是部署用的解码方法，而是用来判断“模型内部是否存在被采样漏掉的可达解”。作者还比较不同 graft 类型的 correct set 重叠，用 fix-set Jaccard 检查这些扰动是否只是同一条轴上的重复探索。","experiments":"实验覆盖 4 个开源 instruction-tuned 模型：Qwen-2.5-3B、Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-Nemo-12B；任务包括 GSM8K、MATH 和 MMLU-Pro。单次解码上，greedy 与平均 single-sample accuracy 持平或更好，最高差距约 2.8 个百分点。对 k=6 的采样，pass@6=0 的题目层在 GSM8K 上占 5.1% 到 8.3%，在 MATH 上占 28.7% 到 43.5%。在这些采样完全失败的样本上，6 条确定性链，也就是 greedy 加 5 种 graft，能在 GSM8K 和 MATH 的 8 个 model-benchmark cell 中恢复 10.3% 到 22.9%；如果包含全部 12 个 cell，恢复范围约为 10% 到 29%。greedy alone 在这些 hardest stratum 上最多只解出约 6% 到 9%，说明收益主要来自确定性扰动的覆盖扩展。不同 graft 类型的 fix-set Jaccard 在所有 cell 中通常不超过 0.47，只有同轴 random probe pair 在一个设置中达到 0.76，支持“不同扰动触达不同可解子集”的解释。","reading_guide":"建议泛读偏精读，约 25 到 35 分钟。Introduction 的三条 empirical observations 是主线，先看清楚作者并不是声称 deterministic decoding 总体优于 sampling，而是指出二者 correct set 部分不重合。表 3 值得看，用来理解 pass@6=0 stratum 的规模。Fig. 3 和附录 C 展示采样边际收益递减，可判断该现象是否只是 k 太小。Section 5 是技术重点，说明 activation grafting 如何构造确定性链，以及恢复比例如何随 deterministic budget 增长。附录 F 的 Jaccard 分析适合检查不同 graft 是否提供了真正不同的覆盖。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper examines a blind spot in using pass@k as a difficulty signal for math reasoning. If no temperature-sampled chain solves a problem, the problem may not be intrinsically hard for the model; it may simply be unreached by the stochastic decoding paths used to estimate difficulty.","importance":"This is relevant to the interface between pretraining, reasoning post-training, and data curation. pass@k is widely used in RL with verifiable rewards, math-data filtering, synthetic curricula, verifier training, and hardness annotation. If a nontrivial part of the pass@k=0 bucket is reachable by the same model through another axis of exploration, then sampling-only pipelines can mislabel reachable examples as unsolvable. That affects which examples are dropped, upweighted, downweighted, or assigned to hard buckets. The contribution is diagnostic rather than a new inference recipe: sampling diversity does not fully characterize the model’s reachable solution space.","technical_approach":"The study compares two matched-compute regimes. The first is standard temperature sampling with k=6 chains, where examples solved by no chain form the hardest stratum. The second is a deterministic regime: greedy decoding plus five activation-grafting variants. Activation grafting replaces the hidden state at the last prompt token with cheap synthetic vectors and then continues with greedy decoding. This keeps the prompt and weights fixed while perturbing the deterministic trajectory through the residual stream. The method is used as a diagnostic tool, not as a recommended deployment decoder. The paper also measures overlap between the sets fixed by different graft types using fix-set Jaccard scores, to test whether the perturbations explore distinct regions rather than repeating the same trajectory.","experiments":"Experiments use four open-weight instruction-tuned models: Qwen-2.5-3B, Llama-3.2-3B, Llama-3.1-8B, and Mistral-Nemo-12B. Benchmarks include GSM8K, MATH, and MMLU-Pro. At one sample, greedy decoding is competitive with or better than the mean single-sample accuracy, with gaps up to about 2.8 percentage points. Under k=6 sampling, the pass@6=0 stratum is 5.1% to 8.3% on GSM8K and 28.7% to 43.5% on MATH. On this no-sample-solves slice, the six-chain deterministic regime recovers 10.3% to 22.9% of examples across the eight free-form math cells, and roughly 10% to 29% across all twelve cells. Greedy alone solves only up to about 6% to 9% of this stratum, so the gain comes from diversified deterministic perturbations. Fix-set Jaccard scores are at most 0.47 in every cell for most graft-type pairs, with one same-axis random-probe pair reaching 0.76, supporting the claim that different grafts reach partially distinct correct sets.","reading_guide":"Skim with attention to the empirical logic; expect about 25 to 35 minutes. the","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T02:26:05Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19714.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.AI","cs.LG","stat.CO","stat.ME"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 paper studies auditing for LLM-as-a-judge pairwise decisions under limited human verification. The target is not to mimic the LLM judge, but to estimate whether each judge preference would agree with a human preference and to decide which uncertain comparisons should be sent for human review. The hard part is that the initial trusted set may itself be biased by the judge being audited, so treating it as a fixed clean positive set can preserve the original error pattern.","importance":"The contribution is most relevant to evaluation infrastructure around pretraining and post-training. Modern model development increasingly uses LLM judges for checkpoint selection, preference data filtering, reward-model data construction, and regression testing. If the judge has systematic biases, those biases can leak into training decisions. AURA is useful because it frames judge reliability as a latent human-consistency variable and couples estimation with selective human verification. This is a practical way to reduce over-reliance on judge scores when human labels are scarce.","technical_approach":"AURA represents each example by the prompt, two candidate responses, the LLM judge preference, and an optional human preference. It defines a human-consistency label y=1 when the LLM preference matches the human preference. For unverified examples, the method estimates P(y=1 | prompt, responses, judge decision). It maintains soft responsibilities for human consistency, anchor confidence for reliable examples, and carried evidence inflow from propagated signals. The loop alternates between training a human-consistency scorer, updating local and anchor-based evidence, propagating reliable signals through a sparse transport refinement over the learned geometry, and selecting informative uncertain examples for human verification. Compared with standard PU learning, the positive and uncertain groups are not fixed. Compared with simple judge calibration, the method explicitly uses anchor propagation and active verification.","experiments":"The evaluation is organized around synthetic recovery, real pairwise LLM evaluation, robustness to noisy initialization, and annotation efficiency. However, most existing MLLMs rely on autoregressive generation, which limits their efficiency for perception tasks that require captioning multiple regions. In this work, we propose PerceptionDLM, a multimodal diffusion language model optimized for efficient parallel region perception. Built upon PerceptionDLM-Base, a strong foundational baseline that achieves state-of-the-art performance am","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T19:27:55Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19534.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但有代表性的建模问题：多模态模型在区域感知任务上，往往需要对同一张图里的多个框或 mask 分别生成描述，而主流 AR MLLM 只能按“区域一个个来、token 一个个出”的方式解码，区域数一多，延迟和吞吐都会线性恶化。作者想验证的是，离散 diffusion language model 能否在保持较强视觉理解质量的同时，把多区域 captioning 改成一次并行去噪完成，从而把 DLM 的并行生成优势真正落到感知任务上，而不是只停留在通用文本生成层面。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的意义主要有两点。第一，它把 diffusion LM 从“能不能做多模态”推进到“在哪类任务上比 AR 更合适”，给出了一个较清楚的适用场景：当输出天然由多个相对独立但又共享图像上下文的片段组成时，非自回归并行生成有结构性优势。第二，它不是只做推理技巧，而是围绕建模目标、输入组织方式、注意力结构和评测协议一起设计，这对研究 AR 之外的新语言建模范式很有参考价值。它未必说明 DLM 会全面替代 AR MLLM，但说明在 region-level perception 这类任务上，AR 的顺序约束可能是多余的，甚至是主要瓶颈。","technical_approach":"方法分两层：先做一个更强的 diffusion VLM 基线，再在其上加入并行区域感知机制。\n\n第一层是 PerceptionDLM-Base。它沿用离散 diffusion language modeling 的基本形式：把文本 token 在前向过程中逐步替换成 [MASK] 之类的吸收态，训练模型在给定 x_t 的条件下恢复原始 token x_0，损失只算被 mask 的位置。多模态部分则是把预训练视觉编码器接到 diffusion language backbone 上，通过 visual instruction tuning 学会根据图像和指令做文本去噪生成。作者把这部分定位成一个更强的 open discrete diffusion VLM baseline，目的是先把“扩散式多模态模型本身太弱”这个问题压下去，否则后面的并行区域机制没有比较基础。\n\n第二层是区域并行机制。核心思想不是把多个区域简单拼成更长 prompt，而是显式告诉模型“哪些 token 属于哪个区域描述”，并限制不同区域生成时的相互干扰。具体看，作者引入了 region-aware mask embeddings，让不同区域对应的被 mask 文本槽位带有区域身份；同时设计 structured attention masking，使每个区域描述在解码时既能访问共享的图像与指令上下文，又不会无约束地串扰到其他区域的文本内容。这样做的原因很直接：如果完全共享文本上下文，多个区域描述容易互相污染；如果完全隔离，又会损失图像级共享语义和指令条件。结构化注意力是在这两者之间取平衡。\n\n从建模角度看，这个方案利用了 DLM 的两个性质。其一，token 级并行去噪允许多个区域的文本槽位同时更新，而不需要像 AR 那样为每个区域单独跑一遍解码。其二，扩散过程天然适合“部分已知、部分待填”的条件生成，区域提示、mask 和图像特征可以统一进同一个 denoising 框架。作者还提出 ParaDLC-Bench，把原本单区域的 DLC-Bench 扩展成并发多区域评测，既看 caption 质量，也看效率。这一点很重要，因为如果只看准确率，很难体现 DLM 相对 AR 的真正收益。","experiments":"实验结果主要支持两个结论：基线质量够强，并行区域机制确实带来效率收益且质量可接受。\n\n1. PerceptionDLM-Base 作为 diffusion VLM 基线并不弱。作者在 16 个多模态 benchmark 上比较，称其在 15/16 个任务上优于 LLaDA-V。这说明后续区域并行结果不是建立在一个明显偏弱的 DLM 底座上。\n\n2. 在新提出的 ParaDLC-Bench 上，并行区域 captioning 的质量明显好于现有 diffusion 基线。PerceptionDLM 平均准确率 62.4%，而 LLaDA-V 只有 35.2%，接近翻倍。这里的提升说明仅仅把通用 diffusion VLM 直接拿来做多区域感知是不够的，区域身份建模和结构化注意力是有效的。\n\n3. 与强 AR 区域模型相比，作者给出的结论是“质量接近但效率更好”。摘要和正文前部强调，在模型规模可比的条件下，随着查询区域数增加，PerceptionDLM 避免了 AR 模型近似线性的延迟增长；在稠密感知场景，例如每张图 5 个 mask 时，吞吐最高可提升 3.5 倍。这个结果符合任务结构：AR 需要为每个区域、每个 token 顺序展开，而 DLM 可以把多个区域的 token 槽位放进同一轮或同一组 denoising steps。\n\n4. 从吞吐曲线描述看，作者还专门分析了 region quantity 增长和 constant workload 下的 scaling。虽然这里没有看到全文后半段更细的表格数字，但从引言给出的结论可以推断，PerceptionDLM 的优势不是单点速度，而是区域数上来后增长更平缓。这比单次 latency 更有意义，因为它说明并行化收益来自建模范式，而不是某个工程 trick。\n\n如果从局限看，这篇工作暂时更像“在特定输出结构上证明 diffusion MLLM 的适配性”，而不是证明 DLM 在通用多模态生成上全面优于 AR。区域描述之间仍可能存在依赖，结构化注意力如何在共享上下文与去串扰之间取最优平衡，应该是后续要继续看的点。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看三部分。第一，看 PerceptionDLM-Base 的架构图和训练目标，确认它与 LLaDA-V 这类离散 diffusion VLM 的差别主要在哪里。第二，看 region-aware mask embeddings 和 structured attention masking 的示意图，这是全文真正的方法增量。第三，看 ParaDLC-Bench 的定义和 throughput 对 region 数量的曲线，因为这决定了作者是否真的证明了“并行区域感知”而不是只做了一个新 benchmark 上的小幅提分。\n\n如果只花 25 分钟左右，建议顺序是：Introduction 中关于 AR 瓶颈和任务设定的动机；PerceptionDLM-Base 小节；并行区域机制小节；ParaDLC-Bench 评测协议；最后看主结果表和 throughput 图。阅读时重点关注两个问题：为什么结构化注意力是必要的，而不是简单把多个区域 prompt 拼接起来；以及 3.5x 吞吐提升是在什么区域数、什么比较基线、什么模型规模下得到的。做 diffusion LM 或 unified multimodal pretraining 的人，值得把它当成一个“DLM 在何处比 AR 更自然”的案例来读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific but important modeling gap in multimodal systems: region-level perception often requires describing multiple boxes or masks from the same image, yet most MLLMs still decode in a fully autoregressive way, region by region and token by token. That makes latency and cost grow roughly linearly with the number of queried regions. The question here is whether a discrete diffusion language model can preserve strong visual understanding while turning multi-region captioning into a parallel denoising problem, so that the parallelism of DLMs becomes useful for perception rather than remaining a generic text-generation property.","importance":"For pretraining and modeling research, the main contribution is not just a faster perception model. It provides a concrete use case where diffusion-style language modeling has a structural advantage over next-token AR decoding. When the output is naturally composed of several conditionally related but partially independent text segments, parallel denoising can be a better fit than strict left-to-right generation. That is useful for people studying alternatives to AR language modeling, especially in multimodal settings. The paper does not show that DLMs dominate AR MLLMs in general, but it does make a credible case that AR imposes an unnecessary sequential bottleneck for dense region perception.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"$23","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"用多模态扩散LM替代AR做多区域感知的思路有参考性，重点看结构化注意力掩码的设计，适合做多模态推理效率优化的ablation参考。","en":"The idea of replacing AR with multimodal diffusion LM for multi-region perception is informative; focus on structured attention mask design for multimodal inference efficiency ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yueyi Sun","Yuhao Wang","Jason Li","Ye Tian","Tao Zhang","Jacky Mai","Yihan Wang","Haochen Wang","Jinbin Bai","Ling Yang","Yunhai Tong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multimodal LLM","diffusion language model","parallel decoding","visual perception"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19534","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.19534","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.19584","title":"Language-Instructed Vision Embeddings for Controllable and Generalizable Perception","abstract":"Vision foundation models are typically trained as static feature extractors, placing the burden of task adaptation onto large downstream models. We propose an alternative paradigm: instead of solely feeding visual features into language models, we use language itself to dynamically guide the vision encoder. Our method, Language-Instructed Vision Embeddings (LIVE), leverages language as high-level guidance to produce task-centric embeddings at inference time, removing the need for task-specific r","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T20:39:04Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19584.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是传统 VLM 的“看图再交给大语言模型解释”，而是更前面的一步：能不能把语言指令直接注入视觉编码器，让视觉表征在推理时按任务动态变化。现有视觉基础模型大多输出静态 embedding，后续问“颜色”“类别”“关系”“是否异常”都只能依赖下游 LLM 在固定视觉特征上做二次推理。一旦视觉编码阶段没有保留某些细粒度信息，后面的 LLM 再大也补不回来，常见表现就是视觉幻觉和任务不受控。LIVE 的核心问题因此很明确：如何训练一个可被自然语言指令调制的视觉编码器，使其在零样本条件下直接产出面向当前任务的视觉 embedding，而不是把任务适配压力全部留给下游语言模型。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在两点。第一，它提出了一种“把语言知识蒸馏回视觉编码器”的路线，和主流“冻结视觉塔 + 扩大后端 LLM”形成了清楚对照。这对原生多模态预训练有启发，因为它讨论的是表示学习阶段的控制能力，而不是单纯靠更大的解码器兜底。第二，它把合成指令数据、对比式对齐目标、语言条件视觉编码三者连成了一个可操作 recipe，说明任务条件化表征可以在 encoder 侧学出来，并且能在不依赖大 LLM 推理的情况下改善泛化和幻觉。量级上，这不是通用 LLM 预训练范式的改写，但对 VLM 预训练、视觉 tokenizer/encoder 设计、以及“是否应把更多语义控制前移到感知层”这个问题，给出了比较扎实的实验支持。","technical_approach":"方法上，LIVE 延续了 CLIP/SigLIP 的双塔框架，但把视觉塔从静态编码器改成语言条件编码器。文本侧复用预训练文本编码器 T(·)，把指令 q 编成文本 embedding；视觉侧构造 E_live(x, T(q))，让图像编码过程显式依赖指令语义。训练目标不是生成答案，而是让“图像 + 指令”得到的视觉 embedding 对齐到正确答案的文本 embedding。也就是说，模型学的是一种任务定向的表征空间：给定同一张图，不同指令会把视觉表示拉向不同答案语义。\n\n训练数据来自合成 triplet：图像、指令、答案。论文用 LLM 作为知识源，在 ImageNet 类图像上生成 instruction-response 对，再与图像组成训练样本。这个设计有两个关键点。其一，监督信号不是配对 caption，而是真正带任务意图的 instruction，因此比“图像描述对齐”更接近下游问答和感知控制。其二，答案仍落在文本编码器空间里，因此训练可以继续使用成熟的对比式对齐框架，而不必引入复杂生成头。\n\n损失函数采用 SigLIP 风格的 sigmoid alignment loss，而不是 CLIP 常见的 softmax 对比损失。文中给出的理由是稳定性和效果更好，尤其适合批内正负样本匹配。训练时只优化语言条件视觉编码器，文本编码器作为语义锚点复用。推理时不需要大语言模型，只需输入图像和指令，就能得到任务相关 embedding，后续可直接用于分类、检索、问答匹配等。\n\n从机制上看，这个方法和 Flamingo/LLaVA 一类架构的区别很清楚：后者把语言理解和任务适配主要放在大解码器里，视觉塔多半仍是通用静态特征提取器；LIVE 则把任务控制前移到视觉编码阶段。它也不同于 caption-conditioned 视觉调制工作，因为这里的文本不是描述图像内容，而是指定“该关注什么”。这使它更接近 instruction-following，而不是 image-text matching 的小改版。","experiments":"实验结论比较集中，重点是“更小的系统，靠 encoder 侧任务化表征，能否替代 LLM-heavy 方案的一部分能力”。论文声称，LIVE 在 MMVP 上把视觉幻觉指标改善了 34 个点；在 GQA 上超过参数量大得多的 LLM-based 方法 7 个点，同时参数量不到对方的 10%。另一个值得注意的结果是，作者把 LIVE 与其作为知识源的 LLM 做 instruction-following 能力对比，称在若干指令跟随基准上把与知识源之间的差距最多缩小了 49 个点。这说明蒸馏并非只学到粗粒度类别信息，而是学到了一部分“按指令选择视觉证据”的能力。\n\n从论文叙述看，实验不只做了最终任务分数，还包含注意力和检索可视化，用来证明视觉编码器内部确实出现了随指令变化的关注区域和表征迁移。这类分析对判断方法是否真的在 encoder 内形成 controllable perception 很关键，因为单看任务分数，仍可能怀疑收益来自数据偏置或答案空间约束。\n\n消融方面，值得重点看三类：一是 instruction 数据相对 caption 数据的作用，验证“任务意图监督”是否真比描述性文本更有效；二是不同对齐损失和文本锚点设计对稳定性的影响；三是不同模型规模下，LIVE 相对 LLM-heavy 基线的参数效率。摘要里没有给出完整参数表和每个 benchmark 的细项数字，但从全文主结论看，作者想证明的不是绝对通用性，而是一个更省参数、更低推理成本的感知控制路径。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但方法部分值得认真看。建议先看 Introduction，重点抓住作者批评现有 VLM 的那条逻辑链：静态视觉特征会把任务适配压力推给后端 LLM，而后端无法恢复前端丢失的信息。接着看 Method 里 E_live(x, T(q)) 的定义和训练目标，确认它不是生成式 VQA，而是 answer-semantic alignment。这里要特别留意为什么答案端仍然放在文本 embedding 空间，以及为什么选 sigmoid loss。\n\n实验部分建议优先看三类图表：MMVP/GQA 主结果表、与知识源 LLM 的 gap 分析、以及 attention/retrieval 可视化。前两者决定方法是否有实际收益，后者决定收益是否来自作者声称的机制。若时间有限，30 分钟左右可以抓住主线；若想深挖，建议继续看合成数据构造细节和消融，判断这种 recipe 是否能迁移到更大规模原生多模态预训练，或者是否受限于答案空间和 instruction 模板质量。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific weakness in current VLM pipelines: vision encoders are usually static, while task adaptation is deferred to a downstream LLM. That design is expensive and often brittle, because the LLM can only reason over whatever visual details the encoder preserved. If the encoder failed to represent the attribute or relation relevant to the query, scaling the decoder does not recover it. LIVE asks whether language instructions can directly steer the vision encoder at inference time, so that the encoder itself produces task-centric embeddings rather than generic visual features.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is not in replacing LLM pretraining, but in shifting where controllability is learned. The work argues that some of the semantic control usually handled by a large multimodal decoder can be moved into the representation-learning stage. That matters for native multimodal pretraining, because it suggests a different allocation of model capacity: less reliance on a heavy language backend, more task-aware perception in the encoder. It also provides a concrete recipe that combines synthetic instruction data, contrastive alignment, and language-conditioned visual encoding. alignm","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T10:52:08Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2606_20076.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"扩散模型的视觉 tokenizer 通常是固定压缩率，导致“质量-算力”只能靠换模型/换 tokenizer 版本来适配不同预算。已有 variable-length tokenizer 多用 nested dropout 截断有序 token 序列，带来跨长度的表示/分布漂移（同一图像在不同 token 数下的相似性结构变化很大），使得单个 diffusion transformer 难以在多长度 latent 上共享一个稳定的 score/denoiser。论文要解决的是：如何设计一种可变长度视觉 tokenizer，使不同长度下的 latent 表示尽量对齐，从而让同一个扩散模型真正实现可控的 token 数（算力）与生成质量折中。","importance":"对“视觉 tokenization + diffusion transformer”的预训练范式有直接启发：问题不在于能不能变长，而在于变长机制是否引入不可忽略的 cross-length distribution shift。把长度调制从“截断”改成“合并”，并显式最小化可度量的表示漂移，给出了一个更适合 diffusion（需要学习全局一致 score 场）的 VLT 设计路径。对做统一多预算生成模型、以及未来把视觉 tokenizer 纳入更大规模多模态预训练（统一 token 空间、统一 backbone）都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"核心思路是用“token merging”而不是“token truncation”来实现变长。\n1) 变长带来的对齐目标：作者把 representational alignment 具体化为“不同长度下 datapoint-wise similarity structure 尽量一致”。截断式 VLT 会把语义/细节按位置分层编码，短序列只保留高层语义，长序列补细节，导致样本间相对关系随长度显著改变。\n2) 合并式长度调制：从 full-length tokens 出发，通过合并若干 token 得到更短序列。关键是：如果扩散模型知道合并模式（哪些 token 被合并到一起），就可以把短序列映射回一个“full-length 等价表示”（cardinality 对齐），从而定义并度量 cross-length representation shift（文中提到 Eq.5 / Sec.3.1）。这样就能把“对齐”变成可优化目标：倾向合并相似 token，可直接降低 shift。\n3) Learnable global merging：生成时没有输入图像，不能用数据依赖的合并（如根据当前图像特征做动态聚类）。作者提出数据无关、全局可学习的合并模式：用可学习的全局 embedding 决定合并关系，并用 straight-through 之类的离散化技巧让合并模式可训练、可在生成时复用。\n4) 与 diffusion transformer 的接口：扩散模型需要感知合并后的 token 的“空间/位置信息”和“合并比例”。作者引入 merged positional embeddings，并配合 proportional attention（让注意力/聚合与合并的 token 覆盖范围或权重成比例）来减少合并带来的位置语义损失。\n5) 训练与使用方式：同一个 diffusion transformer 在训练时看到不同 token 数的 latent（来自不同合并程度），从而实现推理时按预算选择 token 数。另提供 length-specific LoRA 的轻量后训练，用极小参数/成本进一步改善不同预算点的质量。","experiments":"论文主实验在 ImageNet 256×256 生成，指标是 gFID 与计算量（compute）的 trade-off。结论是：合并式 VLT + 对应的 diffusion transformer 训练，在相同 compute 下 gFID 更低，或在相同 gFID 下 compute 更省，优于基于 nested dropout 的现有 VLT。\n消融与分析重点围绕：\n- 合并 vs 截断：验证 cross-length shift/相似性结构漂移显著减小，扩散训练更稳定、跨长度泛化更好。\n- global merging 的可行性：尽管合并模式数据无关，仍能在重建质量与对齐之间取得足够好的折中。\n- merged positional embedding / proportional attention 的必要性：去掉后质量-算力曲线变差。\n- length-specific LoRA：在几乎不增加训练/参数开销的情况下，把若干预算点再往下推一截（更好的 gFID）。\n（注：你提供的全文片段未包含具体数值表格；若需要“具体数字（gFID/compute/参数量）”，需要补充论文的实验表/附录页面。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25-35 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 里对 nested dropout 截断导致 cross-length shift 的机制解释，这是整篇的动机核心。\n- 精看 Sec.3.1（文中提示 Eq.5）：他们如何把“短序列”通过合并模式变成“full-length 等价表示”，以及 shift 的定义方式；这是方法是否站得住的关键。\n- 看 learnable global merging 的训练细节：straight-through 如何用、合并模式参数化方式、以及它如何同时兼顾重建与“合并相似 token”。\n- 看 diffusion quality–compute trade-off and often forces multiple model variants for different budgets. Existing variable-length tokenizers typically use nested-dropout-style truncation of an ordered token sequence, but truncation induces a strong cross-length representation/distribution shift: the same image’s latent similarity structure changes substantially with token count, making it hard for a single diffusion transformer to share one consistent score/denoiser across lengths. The paper targets a variable-length tokenizer design that keeps representations aligned across lengths so one diffusion model can operate effectively at multiple token budgets.","importance":"The main contribution is a diagnosis and a fix that are specific to diffusion training: variable length is not enough; the length modulation mechanism must avoid creating a length-conditioned latent distribution that is too different to share a single score field. This paper studies whic","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T21:19:01Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19607.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","stat.AP"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在偏好数据标注预算固定的情况下，每个 prompt 应该从候选回复池中选择哪些 pair 送去标注，才能让 DPO 等偏好后训练得到的最终策略在 KL 正则化的 RLHF 目标下最优。","importance":"把偏好数据构造从“生成几条、标完所有 pair”的工程惯例，变成一个有明确统计目标的采样设计问题。核心价值在于：标注预算的分配不应该以“估计 reward 准不准”为目标，而应该以“最终策略 J 值高不高”为目标。这对做 DPO/RLHF 数据管线、尤其做大规模离线偏好数据策划的团队有直接参考意义，对理解 post-train 信号质量如何反哺预训练范式也有方法论价值。","technical_approach":"将比较对选择建模为 offline sampling design：给定每个 prompt 下从 reference policy 生成的一批候选 completion，设计一个 pair 上的采样分布，在总标注预算 n 下抽 pair 打标，再用 DPO 训练策略。作者在 KL-regularized RLHF/DPO 的局部线性化近似下，推导出最终策略的次优 gap 由一个依赖于 design 的 Fisher/信息矩阵控制，并给出有限样本上界与信息论下界匹配的结果。由此得到一个可优化的 trace-form 准则，用来选择最能识别“对最终策略性能重要的参数方向”的 pair。区别于主动学习类工作按 reward uncertainty 选 pair，这里的选对准则直接以下游 policy improvement 为目标。","experiments":"在 synthetic tabular、contextual bandit 风格实验，以及 IMDb、Anthropic-HH 的 DPO 实验上验证该准则。报告显示所提 design 在相同标注预算下 consistently 提升 DPO 的 sample efficiency，优于随机采样、全 pair 标注以及已有 active preference selection 基线。论文主要验证的是 offline curation 场景：候选池先生成好、再选 pair，而非在线交互式查询。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看问题建模、信息矩阵与 trace 准则的推导直觉，以及实验部分对不同 curation 策略的对比。建议先读 Introduction 和主要定理对应的解释段，跳过过细的证明；关注“为什么以最终策略目标选 pair 不同于以 reward 估计精度选 pair”这一点。预计阅读时间 30–45 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Given a fixed budget for human preference labels, which completion pairs should be selected from a larger candidate pool per prompt so that the resulting DPO-style policy best optimizes the KL-regularized RLHF objective?","importance":"The paper reframes preference-data construction from an ad hoc pipeline choice—generate a few responses and label all or one pair—to a statistical sampling-design problem. The key conceptual move is that labeling budget should be allocated to maximize downstream policy value under RLHF, not merely to estimate a reward function accurately. This is directly relevant to teams building DPO/RLHF data pipelines, especially offline preference curation at scale, and provides a useful lens for reasoning about post-training signal quality more broadly.","technical_approach":"The problem is formulated as offline experimental design: for each prompt, a pool of completions is first sampled from the reference policy, then a sampling distribution over within-prompt pairs determines which pairs are sent for annotation under budget n, and DPO is trained on the labeled pairs. Under a local linearization of the KL-regularized RLHF/DPO objective, the authors show that the final policy’s optimality gap is governed by a design-dependent information matrix. They derive finite-sample upper bounds and matching information-theoretic lower bounds, leading to an explicit trace-form criterion that selects pairs informative for the parameter directions that most affect downstream policy performance. Unlike active-preference work that selects pairs by reward uncertainty, the selection criterion here is tied directly to policy improvement.","experiments":"The method is validated on synthetic tabular and contextual experiments, plus DPO experiments on IMDb and Anthropic-HH. The proposed design consistently improves sample efficiency over baselines under matched annotation budgets, including random pair selection, labeling all pairs, and prior active preference-selection strategies. The setting is primarily offline curation: generate a candidate pool first, then choose pairs, rather than fully online interactive querying.","reading_guide":"Skim for the problem formulation and the design criterion rather than the full proofs. The main point to absorb is why optimal pair selection for policy optimization differs from optimal pair selection for reward estimation. Offline, the reward model is trained as a quantile regressor rather than a deterministic scalar predictor. The median is used as the reward estimate, while the calibrated prediction interval width serves as a per-sample uncertainty measure. A conformal calibration step is applied on a held-out set to improve interval coverage. Online, the interval width is interpreted as heteroscedastic observation noise. The method decomposes reward variance into signal and noise terms and derives a reliability weight for each rollout.
