Group-relative methods such as GRPO avoid training a critic, but typically assign a trajectory-level advantage to every token. Actor-critic methods provide denser learning signals, but require a learned value function with its own training instability. We introduce VIMPO, a critic-free","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T09:44:12Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_20008.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"VIMPO 处理的是 RLVR 里一个很现实的矛盾：GRPO 这类 critic-free 方法训练简单、稳定，但通常把同一个 trajectory-level advantage 广播给整条输出的所有 token，信用分配太粗；PPO / actor-critic 方法能给 token-level 信号，但需要单独训练 value function，容易出现 critic 失准、策略与价值网络共适应、训练不稳。作者试图证明，这个二选一并不是必须的：在 KL 正则化的自回归生成框架下，可以从策略本身和参考模型的 log-ratio 推出一个隐式 value recurrence，从而在不训练独立 critic 的情况下恢复更细粒度的 token-level credit assignment。","importance":"这篇工作属于 post-train，但和基座研究的接口很直接。它的价值不在于提出一个更复杂的 RL 管线，而在于重新整理了 KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR 里 policy、reference、reward、value 之间的关系。对做预训练和后训练接口的人来说，重要启发有两点。第一，reference model 不只是一个 KL 约束对象，它还可以成为构造内部学习信号的坐标系。第二，很多所谓 critic-free 方法之所以信用分配差，不是因为没有 value network 就必然做不到，而是因为没有把策略最优性条件用到位。VIMPO 给出了一种中间路线：保留 group rollout 的简单性，同时通过 policy-implied value 恢复 Bellman-consistent 的 token 级监督。这对长链推理、代码生成这类长序列 RL 很有现实意义。","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"从全文给出的信息看，实验重点是和 GRPO 做对比，任务集中在需要可验证奖励的推理场景。作者的主结论有两条：一是 VIMPO 在调好超参后训练上升更快，验证准确率更高；二是在 reward noise 存在时，两者都会退化，但 VIMPO 更稳，说明 token-level、Bellman-consistent 的优势估计比整段广播更不容易被噪声放大。\n\n摘要和正文片段没有给出完整数表，但实验设计逻辑比较明确：会比较训练曲线、最终验证性能，以及 noisy reward 条件下的鲁棒性。若正文中包含 GAE-style 聚合、KL 系数、group size、reference 冻结方式等消融，这些会比单一最终分数更重要，因为 VIMPO 的收益高度依赖 value recurrence 是否稳定、以及 token-level advantage 的方差是否可控。\n\n从研究判断看，这篇的实验说服力主要取决于三点：第一，是否在长输出任务上明显优于 GRPO，而不是只在短答案任务上有小幅提升；第二，是否展示了无需 critic 仍能得到更合理的 token 级 credit，而不是仅靠更强超参；第三，噪声奖励实验是否覆盖不同噪声强度，而不是单点比较。若这些部分做得完整，方法就不只是一个理论改写。","reading_guide":"泛读建议按推导链条来读。\n\n先看把 autoregressive generation 写成 deterministic MDP 的部分，以及 KL-regularized objective 的定义。这里决定了后面 value recurrence 的成立条件。若这一步没看清，后面的“implicit value”会显得像技巧而不是结构性结果。\n\n然后重点看 value recurrence 和 terminal boundary condition V(s_T)=0 的推导。这是整篇最值得读的地方。要确认作者到底推导出了什么量、它和 soft value / standard value 的关系是什么、为什么不需要单独 critic 也能形成 Bellman-consistent 目标。\n\n第三，看 actor update 那一节，尤其是 one-step TD advantage 和 GAE-style 扩展。这里决定它到底是不是一个可用的 RL 算法，而不只是一个 value identity。\n\n最后再看实验。建议优先看训练曲线、reward noise 实验、以及和 GRPO 在长序列任务上的差异。如果时间有限，30 分钟可以抓住主要思想；若要判断是否值得复现，最好再花 20 分钟检查附录里的推导假设和实现细节，比如 KL 形式、group Monte Carlo 估计、以及 advantage 截断或归一化方式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"VIMPO targets a practical tension in RL with verifiable rewards for LLMs. Critic-free methods such as GRPO are simple and often stable, but they usually broadcast the same trajectory-level advantage to every token in a completion, which gives very coarse credit assignment. Actor-critic methods provide token-level learning signals, but they require a separately trained value function and inherit all the usual critic calibration and co-adaptation issues. The paper argues that this trade-off is not necessary: under KL-regularized autoregressive generation, one can derive a policy-implied value recurrence from policy/reference log-ratios and recover token-level credit assignment without learning an explicit critic.","importance":"This is a post-training paper, but it matters for foundation-model researchers because it reorganizes the relationship between policy, reference model, reward, and value in KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR. Two ideas stand out. First, the reference model is not only a KL anchor; it can also serve as the coordinate system from which internal training signals are derived. Second, poor credit assignment in critic-free RL is not an unavoidable consequence of removing the value network. It is partly a consequence of not fully exploiting the optimality structure of the KL-regularized objective. VIMPO offers a middle path: keep the simplicity of grouped rollouts, but recover Bellman-consistent token-level supervision through an implicit value construction. That is especially relevant for long-form reasoning and code generation, where sequence-level broadcasting is a poor fit.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"$2e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇值得看推导和 ablation，不一定改写 RLVR 范式，但如果你在嫌 critic 不稳、又嫌 GRPO 太糙，至少应复现它的 value recurrence 看信号是否真更干净。","en":"Read the derivation and ablations: it may not redefine RLVR, but if you dislike unstable critics and find GRPO too coarse, this implicit value recurrence is worth reproducing to test whether the signal is actually cleaner."},"metadata":{"institutions":["UC Berkeley"],"authors":["Zhewei Kang","Aosong Feng","Sergey Levine","Dawn Song","Xuandong Zhao"],"notable_authors":["Sergey Levine","Dawn Song"],"tags":["RLVR","credit-assignment","critic-free","policy-optimization","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20008","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.20008","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.20475","title":"Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution","abstract":"In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them compa","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T16:54:25Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_20475.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在“离线、分 batch”的 trace distillation 里，同一个记忆操作（memory op）会在不同 batch 上收到相互矛盾的反馈；现有方法缺少跨 batch、以“操作”为粒度的证据累积机制，导致系统难以区分“稳定有效的改进”与“偶然命中”。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接贡献有限，更偏向 agent 记忆/自进化的后处理与训练信号聚合。但它把一个常见工程痛点讲清楚：当监督信号来自噪声较大的 proxy（LLM judge）且数据分布随 batch 漂移时，单 batch 的排序/筛选会系统性过拟合；需要可对齐（identity）与可比（scale）两类结构条件，才能做跨批次的长期信用分配。这种“差分 + 证据累积”的思路，对持续学习/在线数据筛选/合成数据过滤也有可迁移的启发。","technical_approach":"方法名为 Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA)，定位为 distillation 的后处理架构层。\n1) 两个结构条件：\n- Alignability（可对齐）：跨 batch 识别语义等价的操作，并合并到同一累积单元，否则无法累积证据。\n- Comparability（可比）：不同 batch 任务分布不同，必须把每次评估信号变成同尺度、可加的量，否则跨 batch 相加没有意义。\n2) 可对齐的实现：把 memory bank 抽象成带稳定 id 的 item 集合；每个 op 用 (type, anchor id, content embedding) 标识；新候选与已有 op 语义相似度超过阈值则做 semantic identity merging，避免“同义不同表述”导致证据碎片化。\n3) 可比的实现（核心）：对每个候选 op_i，在当前 batch B 上做外层差分：\n- 先用 LLM judge 估计 u(M,B)∈[0,100]，表示当前记忆 M 在 batch B 上的期望效用；\n- 构造应用该 op 后的记忆 M_i，再估计 u(M_i,B)；\n- 定义边际优势 δ_i = u(M_i,B) - u(M,B) ∈ [-100,100]。\n差分把 batch 的“绝对难度/分布差异”尽量抵消，使 δ 更接近 op 的增量贡献，从而可跨 batch 累加。\n4) 证据累积：对每个 op 单元 k，在时间/批次维度上对 δ_{k,t} 做带符号的 EMA 累积，形成稳定的长期证据；论文强调不要求 u 无偏，只要求 δ 的方向与真实增益方向“统计上优于随机”（directional alignment）。\n整体上，MAA把“选择一次”改成“持续记账”：不直接用单次 accept/reject，而是用跨批次的累积证据驱动保留、抑制或回滚某类操作。","experiments":"摘要声称在 4 个 benchmark × 4 个 target model 的 16 组设置里，MAA 在 14/16 组取得最好结果，并且作为 post-processing 架构能稳定超过若干现有 trace distillation 方法（文中点名 SkillOpt、Trace2Skill 等）。但给定你提供的全文片段未包含实验表格与具体数值（提升幅度、置信区间、judge 设定、合并阈值、EMA 超参等），无法在不臆测的前提下复述关键数字与消融结论。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 20–30 分钟）：\n- 重点看 Introduction 对“batch-style distillation 为什么会产生矛盾反馈”的例子与问题抽象，确认它解决的是“跨 batch 信号聚合”而不是更强的在线验证。\n- 精看 §3.1（identity merging 的定义、相似度/阈值、anchor id 设计）与 §3.2–3.3（δ 的构造与 EMA 累积），这是方法是否可复现、是否会引入新偏差的关键。\n- 看实验部分时优先核对三点：① u(M,B) 的 judge 提示词与一致性；② δ 差分是否真的降低 batch 间方差（有没有方差/相关性分析）；③ identity merging 的误合并/漏合并对最终收益的敏感性（阈值消融）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In offline, batch-wise trace distillation for memory-driven agent self-evolution, the same memory operation can receive conflicting feedback across batches. however, it faces a fundamental optimization instability: uniform token updates precipitate entropy collapse, leading to premature convergence to suboptimal strategies, whereas excessive Shannon Entropy maximization can cause entropy explosion, driving blind exploration toward incoherent reasoning chains. To resolve this dichotomy, we introduce the Independent Combinatorial Toke","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T04:11:56Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19771.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"RLVR（带可验证奖励的 RL）训练 LLM 推理时，对所有 token 均匀施加策略梯度容易造成熵塌缩到次优策略；而单纯用 Shannon entropy 做强探索又会导致熵爆炸和无方向的胡乱探索。问题在于只用一个标量熵值无法刻画哪些 token 才是真正决定推理分支的关键位置。","importance":"这篇工作属于 post-train / RL 训练信号设计方向，但对理解“预训练后阶段里哪些 token 承载主要学习信号”有启发。核心观察是：token 层面的重要性不应该只用熵这个不确定性标量衡量，而要看 token 分布相对序列平均分布的偏离程度。这个视角与 reward hacking、credit assignment、稀疏有效更新等问题直接相关。更实际的意义是，他们报告只更新约 10% 的 token 就能匹配或超过全量更新，这对降低 RLVR 计算成本和稳定训练都有价值。","technical_approach":"提出 ICT（Independent Combinatorial Tokens）框架。核心做法是：对每个 token 位置的 logit 分布，计算它与该序列上 token 分布的平均之间的 Jensen-Shannon divergence；JS 散度大的 token，代表其分布形态偏离整体，被视为关键分支点/unique token。训练时只对这些 top-k（如 top 10%）unique token 保留梯度，其余 token 的 policy gradient 被 mask 掉。这样梯度更新集中在真正决定推理路径分岔的位置，而不是对所有 token 一视同仁地推高或压低熵。论文从信息论直觉出发，证明这类分布独特的 token 对应关键 branching points，选择性更新能同时避免 entropy collapse 和 entropy explosion，实现隐式熵控。","experiments":"在 Qwen2.5 系列 0.5B、1.5B、7B 上做数学/代码类 RLVR 实验，覆盖七个推理 benchmark。结果显示只更新 top 10% distributionally unique tokens，性能可以匹配或超过全参数/全 token 更新；相比 GRPO、20-Entropy、STAPO 等强基线，平均 pass@4 提升约 4.37%、4.98%、4.38%，最大增益达 14.9%。论文还做了消融说明 JS 散度选 token 优于直接用 Shannon entropy 选高熵 token：高熵 token 只代表不确定，不代表有明确的有效探索方向。","reading_guide":"泛读为主，重点看 logit 分布偏离的定义、为什么 JS 比 Shannon entropy 更适合选关键 token，以及稀疏梯度只更新 10% token 的实验设置和结果。图 1 关于“相同熵但分布形态不同”的示意值得看，方法部分和实验表格是核心。理论证明部分可粗读，重点抓直觉。预计阅读时间 25–40 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In RL with verifiable rewards for LLM reasoning, applying policy gradients uniformly across all tokens often causes entropy collapse to suboptimal strategies, while indiscriminate Shannon-entropy-based exploration leads to entropy explosion and undirected search. A scalar entropy value is insufficient to identify which tokens actually determine critical reasoning branches.","importance":"This is a post-training/RL credit-assignment paper, but it bears on a broader pretrain-relevant question: which tokens carry the effective learning signal in late-stage policy optimization. The useful observation is that token importance should not be measured by entropy alone; distributional shape relative to surrounding tokens matters. The practical claim is also strong: updating only around 10% of tokens can match or exceed full updates, with implications for RLVR stability and compute efficiency.","technical_approach":"The paper proposes ICT (Independent Combinatorial Tokens). For each token position, ICT computes the Jensen-Shannon divergence between that token’s logit distribution and the sequence-level average token distribution. Tokens with large JS divergence are treated as unique tokens, i.e., critical branching points. During policy optimization, gradients are retained only for these top-k tokens (e.g., top 10%), while other tokens are masked. This concentrates policy-gradient updates on decision points that steer reasoning trajectories, rather than uniformly pushing entropy up or down across all positions. The paper argues theoretically that these distributionally unique tokens encode directional exploration information, enabling implicit entropy control without directly regularizing Shannon entropy.","experiments":"Experiments use Qwen2.5 models from 0.5B to 7B across seven reasoning benchmarks. Updating only the top 10% distributionally unique tokens matches or outperforms full-gradient training. Against strong baselines including GRPO, 20-Entropy, and STAPO, ICT reports average pass@4 gains of 4.37%, 4.98%, and 4.38%, with maximum gains up to 14.9%. Ablations support the claim that JS-based token selection is more effective than selecting high-Shannon-entropy tokens, since high entropy indicates uncertainty but not necessarily useful exploration direction.","reading_guide":"Skim for the token-selection idea and the sparse-update results. The most important sections are the motivation on why entropy is insufficient, the definition of distributional uniqueness via JS divergence, and the experiments showing strong performance with only 10% of token gradients. Figure 1 on equal-entropy but distributionally different logits is a useful intuition pump. Theoretical proofs can be read lightly. transparency，即能否用这些中间状态重构模型如何得到答案。这个拆分对未来 latent reasoning、masked/any-order LM、diffusion LM 的训练设计有直接启发：如果训练或架构把中间状态压向 embedding/token 空间，透明性可能保留；如果大量 outcome-based RL 鼓励模型把有效推理藏进连续 latent，透明性可能下降。对预训练研究来说，贡献不是一个新训练 recipe，而是给非 AR 语言模型提供了一个评估和约束中间计算可读性的框架。","technical_approach":"论文先从 opaque serial depth 入手。这个量衡量模型在两个可解释瓶颈之间能连续做多少串行计算。DiffusionGemma 每个 denoising step 之间的瓶颈包含 C 个自然语言 token 和 C 个 self-conditioning vectors，C 是 canvas 长度。如果不把这些中间 bottleneck 视为可解释，DiffusionGemma 的 opaque serial computation 是对应 Gemma 的 28.6×；如果这些 bottleneck 可解释，则降到 1.1×。随后论文检验这个假设是否成立。它做了两步：第一，限制 denoising step 之间的信息流，把 bottleneck 中可传递的信息压到 O(c) 个自然语言 token，观察性能是否保持；第二，分析这些映射出来的 token 是否对应附近位置的 token guesses。结果显示，大部分信息可以通过接近自然语言 token 的形式表达，且这些 token 往往是在猜测邻近位置，从而支持一定程度的 variable transparency。论文还改造 monitorability evaluation，让外部 monitor 根据 DiffusionGemma 的中间轨迹提取信息，并与 Gemma 4 的 CoT 可监控性对照。最后通过 case studies 分析 diffusion LM 独有的算法现象，包括非时间顺序推理、token/sequence smearing，以及 intermediate-context reasoning。","experiments":"关键数字有三类。第一是 opaque serial depth：在不承认中间 bottleneck 可解释时，DiffusionGemma 的不透明串行深度约为 Gemma 的 28.6×；在承认中间 denoising states 可解释时，只约为 1.1×。第二是 bottleneck 压缩实验：将 inter-step 信息流压缩为 O(c) 个自然语言 token 后，性能只有很小下降，说明 dense self-conditioning vectors 中相当一部分有效信息能被 token-level 表达近似承载。第三是 monitorability：在改造后的 monitorability evaluations 上，DiffusionGemma 与 Gemma 4 表现接近，说明至少在这些任务中，DiffusionGemma 的中间轨迹没有显著弱化监控器的信息获取能力。case studies 不是为了给出单一指标，而是展示非 AR denoising 的计算路径：模型可以先形成后部 token，再反过来影响前部 token；也可能在多个候选 token 或片段之间做连续态混合，这些行为不会自然出现在普通左到右 CoT 中。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Section 2、3、4、5。Section 2 是核心概念，尤其是 opaque serial depth 如何定义，以及 28.6×/1.1× 这两个数字依赖什么假设。Section 3 是最关键的实验证据，重点看信息流压缩到自然语言 token 的方法，以及 mapped tokens 为什么常常是 nearby token guesses。Section 4 用 monitorability 连接 safety/oversight 场景，可看评测改造是否公平。Section 5 的 case studies 值得细读，因为它展示了 diffusion LM 与 AR LM 在“推理过程”上的真实差异。Appendix A.2 中关于 outcome-based RL 可能降低 latent 可解释性的讨论也值得看，它直接关系到 future latent reasoning model 的训练风险。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether DiffusionGemma, a text diffusion language model, preserves the transparency benefits usually associated with autoregressive chain-of-thought reasoning. The difficulty is that DiffusionGemma refines a whole token canvas through repeated denoising steps, and its inter-step state includes both natural-language tokens and dense self-conditioning vectors. Those vectors may carry important reasoning information that is not directly readable.","importance":"This is a high-value paper for researchers studying diffusion LMs, masked or any-order LMs, and latent reasoning. Its main contribution is a concrete decomposition of transparency into variable transparency and algorithmic transparency. Variable transparency asks whether intermediate states can be interpreted. Algorithmic transparency asks whether those states let us reconstruct the computation that produced the final answer. For pretraining research, the lesson is architectural and training-related: DiffusionGemma appears relatively transparent partly because its intermediate representation is biased toward token embeddings. Future latent reasoning models, especially those trained with substantial outcome-based RL, may not retain that property.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"The most important quantitative result is the opaque-serial-depth contrast: 28.6× Gemma if intermediate denoising states are not interpretable, but only 1.1× if they are. The bottleneck experiments show that reducing inter-step information flow to O(c) natural-language tokens causes minimal performance degradation, suggesting that much of the useful state can be represented in token-like form. The mapped tokens are often guesses for nearby token positions, which supports the variable-transparency claim. On adapted monitorability evaluations, DiffusionGemma and Gemma 4 are similarly monitorable, indicating that the denoising trajectory gives monitors roughly comparable access to task-relevant information in these settings. The qualitative case studies are also important: they show that DiffusionGemma can revise earlier tokens using later context, smear probability mass across competing tokens or sequences, and reason through intermediate canvas states in ways that do not map cleanly onto left-to-right CoT.","reading_guide":"Read Sections 2 through 5 carefully. Training follows each model’s chat template; the assistant target is only a single answer digit, and the loss is computed only on answer tokens. No chain-of-thought supervision is used. Existing methods handle only single-direction injection; when multiple semantic directions are superposed without constraints, the model collapses. activation steering 的稳定性问题。单个 steering direction 往往能工作，但多个语义方向同时注入时，模型输出容易退化。作者把退化拆成两个相对独立的来源：一是激活范数逐层累积，导致 hidden state 偏离训练分布；二是不同语义向量不正交，叠加后互相削弱或串扰。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在训练 recipe，而在表征几何和可控生成机制。它给 activation steering 的失败模式提供了较清楚的诊断框架：多方向控制不是简单把多个向量相加，而要同时控制范数稳定性和方向可分性。这个结论对理解 LLM hidden space 的 superposition、特征干涉、层间分布漂移有启发。作为方法，GEMS 仍是 inference-time 叠加后把激活推到训练分布之外。第二，选择 o_proj 注入点，作者认为这是稳定注入的关键位置；在该位置之外注入更容易导致退化。第三，对多个语义方向做正交化，降低 directional interference。第四，用 Gaussian envelope 在连续多层上分配干预强度，而不是只在单层或少数层注入；这个设计对应了语义信息分布在多层的观察。和 ActAdd、CAA、RepE、ITI 相比，GEMS 的重点从单方向控制转到多方向并行控制；和 MSRS、MAT-STEER、K-Steering 相比，它不引入额外训练过程。","experiments":"实验主线是诊断和消融。作者在 Qwen3.5-4B-Base 上分析多方向 steering collapse，并把失败归因到 distributional deviation 和 directional interference。GEMS 能在 dense model 的 10 层以上连续层中并行注入多个语义方向。关键消融结论是：去掉 norm preservation 或不在 o_proj 注入会导致明显退化；不做正交化时，输出更容易出现语义漂移；Gaussian envelope 用来缓和不同层的干预强度差异。文中还给出 3B 到 31B 模型上的定性迁移结果，说明该几何约束可能不强依赖单一模型架构。提供内容中没有足够完整的量化表格和具体分数，因此不宜把结论解读为稳定的跨模型性能保证。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20 到 30 分钟。建议先看 Introduction 中对两个 failure modes 的定义，再看 Motivation 中关于层间分布式语义存储的论证。方法部分重点看四个设计：norm preservation、o_proj injection、orthogonalization、Gaussian envelope。实验部分重点看消融：哪些约束是必要条件，哪些只是提升稳定性的调节项。值得深挖的是作者如何度量“偏离训练分布”和“方向干涉”，因为这决定了该方法能否从经验技巧变成更通用的 hidden-state 控制原则。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why multi-direction activation steering breaks down. Single-vector steering can often shift model behavior, but adding several semantic directions at once tends to degrade generation. The authors decompose the failure into two sources: norm-level distributional deviation, where repeated additive perturbations push hidden states away from the training distribution, and directional interference, where non-orthogonal semantic vectors suppress or distort one another.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model research is mechanistic rather than pretraining procedural. It provides a useful diagnosis of hidden-space control: multi-attribute steering is not just vector addition, because both activation-scale stability and geometric separability matter. This connects to superposition, feature interference, and layer-wise representation drift. GEMS is an inference-time method, so its immediate use is more likely in model analysis, controlled generation, and structured data synthesis than in base-model training itself.","technical_approach":"GEMS is a training-free geometric intervention method. It keeps the intervention within a stable norm range, injects at the o_proj location, orthogonalizes semantic directions to reduce interference, and distributes intervention strength over consecutive layers with a Gaussian envelope. The design follows the observation that semantic information is spread across layers, making single-layer steering brittle. Compared with ActAdd, CAA, RepE, and ITI, the shift is from single-attribute control to parallel multi-attribute control. Compared with learned multi-steering methods such as MSRS, MAT-STEER, and K-Steering, GEMS avoids additional optimization or architecture changes.","experiments":"The diagnostic analysis is conducted on Qwen3.5-4B-Base. The paper reports that multi-direction collapse can be separated into distributional deviation and directional interference, and that GEMS can inject multiple semantic directions across more than 10 consecutive layers in dense models. The key ablations are qualitative but informative: removing norm preservation or moving away from the o_proj injection point leads to immediate collapse; removing orthogonalization causes semantic drift; the Gaussian envelope helps allocate intervention strength across layers. The paper also reports preliminary qualitative transfer across models from 3B to 31B. The provided text does not contain enough complete quantitative tables, so the results should be read as evidence for a design principle rather than a fully established scaling claim.","reading_guide":"A skim is sufficient, about 20 to 30 minutes. The challenge is threefold. A behavioral shift can be easy to observe but hard to localize internally. A separable direction inside one model does not imply causal relevance. Within a model family, the extracted direction is often behaviorally meaningful and fairly specific. Across families, mapped directions can still suppress the behavior, but specificity largely breaks down. That boundary matters for activation steering, alignment auditing, and post-training diagnostics. However, the Turing machine, while suitable for complexity-theoretic analysis, is not convenient, intuitive, or efficient for discussing algorithms. Algorithms are typically designe","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-18T01:49:29Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19697.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"已有结果说明带 chain-of-thought 的 Transformer 可以模拟图灵机，因此具有通用计算能力。但图灵机不是算法设计中常用的高效抽象。该研究要回答的是：CoT Transformer 能否直接、高效地模拟 Word RAM 模型上的算法，从而接近教材算法的复杂度，而不是承受图灵机模拟带来的二次开销。","importance":"这是偏理论的 reasoning expressivity 工作，对预训练 recipe 没有直接实验结论，但对理解“推理 token 能表达什么计算”有帮助。它把 CoT 的计算能力从图灵机层面的可计算性，推进到 Word RAM 层面的算法效率。这个区别重要：图灵机模拟说明能做，但不说明推理长度是否合理；Word RAM 模拟把随机访问、字级算术、比较和位运算纳入模型，使 CoT 长度可以按算法步骤数加 polylog 开销来讨论。需要注意，它主要是存在性构造，不证明模型能通过训练自动学到这些模拟策略。","technical_approach":"研究给出三类直接模拟 Word RAM 的构造。第一类是有限精度、polylog 宽度的 decoder-only CoT Transformer，使用 rightmost-unique hard attention。它按 Word RAM 指令级别生成 CoT，而不是逐步模拟图灵机 tape movement。若 Word RAM 运行 t 步、word size 为 w = Θ(log(n+t))，一般指令集每条指令需要 O(w^2) 个 CoT token，总长度为 O(t log^2(n+t))；去掉乘法相关操作后，每条指令开销降为 O(w)，总长度为 O(t log(n+t))。第二类是固定宽度、continuous CoT 的 Transformer，用隐藏向量而非离散 token 承载中间状态，并允许每条 Word RAM 指令调用一个图灵机 oracle。第三类是 Transformer 加 linear RNN 层的 hybrid model，可以用显式 CoT 实现同类模拟。对单条指令在图灵机上耗时 κ(w) 的指令集，后两类构造需要 O(tκ(w)) 个 CoT 步。研究还给出离散 CoT 的下界：固定字母表要表示 w-bit word 至少需要 Ω(w) 个 token，因此总开销至少 Ω(tw)。这解释了为什么 O(t log(n+t)) 已接近可期待的离散 CoT 下限。","experiments":"没有常规经验实验，主要结果是复杂度构造和下界证明。关键数字是相对于图灵机路径的复杂度改进：通过先把 Word RAM 模拟成图灵机，再让 CoT 模拟图灵机，随机访问会带来 O(t^2) 级别的开销；直接 Word RAM 模拟把开销降到 O(t log^2(n+t))，在不含乘法、除法、取模的指令集下为 O(t log(n+t))。文中用排序和 Dijkstra 这类算法作为动机：它们在 Word RAM 上有熟悉的复杂度界，而图灵机模拟会人为放大随机访问成本。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 到 45 分钟。先看 Word RAM 定义和问题设定，确认 w = Θ(log(n+t))、随机访问和 unit-cost word operations 的假设。然后看主定理和有限精度构造，这是核心创新。下界部分有助于判断 token 开销是否只是构造不够紧。continuous CoT 与 hybrid Transformer + linear RNN 部分可以按兴趣阅读；它们更像是扩展构造，用来说明结果不只依赖一种离散 transcript 设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Prior work shows that chain-of-thought transformers can simulate Turing machines, but that result is too low-level to explain efficient algorithm execution. The central question is whether CoT transformers can simulate Word RAM algorithms directly, preserving the complexity style used in textbook algorithms rather than paying the overhead of tape-based Turing machine simulation.","importance":"This is a theory-oriented contribution for reasoning models. Its value is not a new training method, but a sharper model of what explicit or latent reasoning traces can represent efficiently. Turing completeness only says that computation is possible. A Word RAM simulation says that random access memory, word-level arithmetic, comparisons, and bit operations can be represented with only polylogarithmic overhead in the reasoning trace. The result should be read as an expressivity statement, not as evidence that current models will learn these simulations from data.","technical_approach":"$38","experiments":"There are no empirical model-training experiments. The results are asymptotic constructions and lower bounds. The main quantitative comparison is against the Turing-machine route: simulating a t-step Word RAM computation through a Turing machine can require O(t^2) CoT steps because random memory access becomes tape scanning. The direct simulation reduces this to O(t log^2(n+t)) in the general finite-precision setting and O(t log(n+t)) for multiplication-free instruction sets. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. 权重幅值分布用 Weibull 两参数拟合时，scale 参数 λ 在 AdamW 训练中为什么会稳定地经历“上升—过冲—回落—平台”这条轨迹。难点不在于再做一次经验观察，而在于把参数级优化器动力学和矩阵级分布统计量接起来。AdamW 直接作用在单个参数及其一二阶矩上，而 λ 是整个权重矩阵元素幅值分布的汇总量。论文试图回答：究竟是哪些优化力在推动 λ 上升，哪些力又让它停止并回落，以及这种解释能否在没有 optimizer states 的公开 checkpoint 上近似恢复。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它把“权重尺度如何形成”从经验描述推进到可分解的动力学解释，给 AdamW 下的 weight norm / weight scale 演化提供了一个较清楚的力学图景：早期主要是 update 与当前权重的对齐项在推高尺度，后期则是对齐项与 decoupled weight decay 接近平衡。这个结论对理解学习率、weight decay、warmup 以及训练后期 cooldown 为什么会改变终态权重尺度是有帮助的。第二，它提出了一个从稀疏 checkpoint 反推 force budget 的近似方法。虽然这还不是直接改进训练 recipe 的工作，但对做训练诊断、比较不同数据配比或不同优化超参下的训练动态，有一定工具价值。贡献量级偏分析与诊断，不是 recipe 级突破。","technical_approach":"方法上分三步。第一步，从 AdamW 的 decoupled update 出发，推导平方权重范数的 leading-order 演化式，并把它拆成三项“力”：alignment force、injection force、decay force。alignment force 衡量当前权重与自适应更新方向 u_hat 的相关性；如果更新方向在权重方向上有较强投影，就会推动范数增长。injection force 来自自适应步长本身的二阶注入项，可以理解为 update magnitude 对范数的正向灌入。decay force 则是 decoupled weight decay 对范数的线性拉回。第二步，建立 norm-level 动力学与 Weibull λ 的桥。作者依赖前作观察到的 k-lock：对一类 transformer 组件，Weibull shape 参数 k 在训练中近似锁定在初始化附近，因此 RMS 与 λ 之间只差一个近似常数因子 sqrt(Gamma(1+2/k))。这样就能把平方范数的动力学近似映射到 λ 的轨迹，同时显式测量 RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset，而不是把两者硬当成同一个量。第三步，处理现实中拿不到 AdamW moments 的情况。作者基于 AdamW displacement identity 设计 spline displacement method，用多 checkpoint 的样条位移近似恢复 update 方向，再估计 alignment force。这个设计比朴素两点差分更稳，因为它试图利用稀疏时间序列恢复局部位移方向，而不是把相邻 checkpoint 的粗糙差值直接当作真实 update。与已有工作相比，区别在于它不是只讨论终态 norm 与 lr/wd 的关系，也不是只做权重分布拟合，而是把 optimizer-level force decomposition 和 distribution-level λ 连接起来。","experiments":"实验主体基于自训练的 Pythia-70M，并保留 ground-truth optimizer moments 以便直接测量三种 force。关键结果有三点。其一，在 rise phase，alignment force 明显主导绝对 force budget，四个随机种子上贡献约 88%–94%。这说明 λ 的早期增长不是主要由 step magnitude 的 injection 项驱动，而是由“更新方向与已有权重方向的对齐”驱动。其二，在接近饱和和过冲后回落阶段，alignment 与 decay 逐渐接近平衡，这与 λ 从增长转为回落的时间点对应，给出了 overshoot/relaxation 的动力学解释。其三，作者做了 super-weight removal 后结论仍基本稳健，说明主结论不是由少数极端大权重支配。对于无 moments 的恢复实验，spline displacement method 在自训练模型上可恢复约 92%–94% 的 alignment force，约为 naive two-point baseline 的 2 倍。论文还报告了一个探索性现象：训练数据 coherence 似乎会影响 λ 的绝对峰值，但作者明确承认这里没有做受控 mixture 实验，因此只能视为提示性结果，不能当成已证实的数据规律。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先抓三处。第一，看引言和方法里关于 three-force decomposition 的推导，重点理解 alignment / injection / decay 三项各自对应 AdamW update 的哪一部分，以及为什么作者只强调 leading-order。第二，看把 RMS 连接到 Weibull λ 的桥接部分，尤其是 k-lock 假设成立的条件和 reconstruction offset 的定义；如果这一步不成立，后面的 λ 解释就会变弱。第三，看 sparse-checkpoint recovery 的验证实验，判断这个方法在真实公开模型分析里是否足够可靠。若时间有限，优先看 Figure 1、force budget 主图、以及 recovery accuracy 对比图，20–30 分钟可以抓住主线。更细一点可留意作者如何定义 rise / saturation 阶段，以及 super-weight removal 的鲁棒性检验是否足够严格。整体上，这篇更适合作为训练动力学诊断工具来读，而不是直接寻找可落地的新 recipe。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper explains a specific but recurring training-level phenomenon: when transformer weight magnitudes are fit by a two-parameter Weibull distribution, the scale parameter λ tends to rise, overshoot, relax, and then settle under AdamW training. The central problem is to connect optimizer-level dynamics, defined on individual parameters and Adam moments, to a matrix-level distributional statistic. The question is not merely whether λ tracks weight growth, but which terms in AdamW actually drive the rise, which terms stop it, and whether those forces can be recovered when only sparse public checkpoints are available.","importance":"Its value is mainly diagnostic and mechanistic. First, it gives a more concrete account of how weight scale is formed under AdamW: early growth is dominated by alignment between the current weights and the adaptive update direction, while later dynamics are governed by a balance between that alignment term and decoupled weight decay. That is useful for thinking about learning rate, weight decay, warmup, and late-stage cooldown, because all of them affect the final weight scale through this balance. Second, it proposes a practical checkpoint-only recovery method for the force budget. This is not a new training recipe, but it is a potentially useful tool for comparing optimization regimes, data mixtures, or training phases when optimizer states are unavailable.","technical_approach":"$3a","experiments":"$3b","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three pieces. First, read the derivation of the three-force decomposition and make sure each term is tied to a specific part of the AdamW update; the leading-order assumption matters. Second, read the RMS-to-Weibull bridge, especially the role of k-lock and the reconstruction offset. If that bridge is weak, the interpretation of λ becomes much less convincing. Third, inspect the sparse-checkpoint recovery section, since that determines whether the method is useful beyond self-trained runs with optimizer states. If time is limited, Figure 1, the main force-budget plots, and the recovery-accuracy comparison are enough to capture the paper’s main idea in about 20–30 minutes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\\|γ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-17T18:28:37Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_19491.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注的是 LayerNorm Transformer 中一种可由参数直接读出的“死方向”诊断。更具体地说，预训练模型常处在 Fisher 信息退化的奇异极小值附近，存在一些参数方向上 loss 几乎不变。通常要定位这类方向，需要大量前向或前后向计算，或者做激活协方差的特征分解。作者要解决的问题是：对于带 LayerNorm 的 Transformer，能否仅从模型参数本身、甚至只看 LN 的 scale 参数 γ，就构造出一个理论上必然落在 post-final-norm activation covariance 核空间中的方向，并把它作为大模型尺度上的 dead direction 诊断。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接启发不如优化或数据论文那样强，但它在训练后结构诊断上有一个很少见的优点：给出 closed-form、forward-pass-free 的退化方向。若结论可靠，这意味着某些奇异性和低曲率结构并不需要昂贵的 Hessian/Fisher 近似才能看到，而是由架构中的归一化代数结构直接决定。对做训练动力学、loss landscape、局部学习系数、flatness 诊断的人，这提供了一个很便宜的 baseline：先区分哪些退化是 LayerNorm 几何必然带来的，哪些是训练进一步加深出来的。对预训练 recipe 本身，它更像分析工具，而不是直接可转化为性能提升的方法。","technical_approach":"核心技术点是一个代数结论。对 LayerNorm 而言，均值中心化会引入一个投影结构；结合 affine scale γ 后，作者证明 inverse-scale direction，也就是 γ^{-1}/||γ^{-1}||，是 post-final-norm centered activation covariance 的精确核方向，而且这个结论对任意输入分布都成立。换句话说，这个方向不需要通过数据采样估计，它由 LN 参数代数地决定。随后作者借助 KFAC 风格的 Fisher 因子化，把 residual-stream 中的这个核方向对应到参数空间中的 dead direction。论文同时给出 RMSNorm 的对照结论：因为 RMSNorm 没有 mean-subtraction projector，所以不存在同样的 universal direction。这一点很关键，因为它把现象从“某些模型碰巧如此”变成“LN 与 RMSNorm 两类架构在代数上应当分开”。在实证上，作者又定义了 Schur ratio R(h)=lambda_min(A_l)/(A_l)_{u,u} 及其补量 Δγ=1-R(h)，用来衡量训练后的最小特征方向是否比理论预测的 γ^{-1} 方向还要更深。这样就把“架构给出的代数基线”和“训练额外挖深的退化”分开了。","experiments":"实验覆盖 14 个预训练 Transformer，其中 9 个是 LayerNorm，5 个是 RMSNorm。对 9/9 个 LN 模型，在 random initialization 时，预测方向 v*=γ^{-1}/||γ^{-1}|| 与实测的 post-final-norm centered activation covariance 最小奇异方向 u_perp 的余弦对齐达到 |cos|≥0.9999，平均超过 0.99999，说明代数预测几乎精确命中。对 5/5 个 RMSNorm 模型，同样的对齐只落在随机方向基线附近，中心化协议下 |cos|≤0.10，符合“RMSNorm 不应存在该 universal direction”的理论预期。作者还在 Pythia 的 LN 尺度 sweep 上比较随机初始化与训练后 checkpoint 的 Δγ。随机初始化时，h=512 的 Δγ 约 0.37，较大 hidden size 上基本在 0 附近波动；训练后则在所有尺寸上接近 1，作者将其解释为训练沿同一坐标把谱底进一步挖深，量级约可达 10^3 倍。这个实验的价值不在于证明 dead direction 存在，而在于把“架构必然退化”和“训练加深退化”区分开。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看理论命题本身：LayerNorm 为什么会给出 γ^{-1} 这个方向，RMSNorm 为什么不会。只要这两个命题站住，整篇文章的主线就成立。然后看 14 个模型上的四位小数对齐实验，这是最直接的验证。最后看 Δγ 的定义和 Pythia sweep，理解作者如何把 random-init 的代数基线与 trained checkpoint 的额外退化分开。若只是想判断这篇是否与你的工作相关，20 分钟足够：读引言、命题与证明草图、主实验图表即可。若你做 loss landscape / Fisher / LLC 诊断，可以再细看 centered protocol、Schur ratio 的构造，以及 transformers can be identified directly from parameters, without any forward pass, backward pass, sampling procedure, or activation eigendecomposition. The target object is a Fisher-degenerate direction associated with singular minima. The specific claim is that for LayerNorm-equipped transformers, one can read off a universal residual-stream direction from the LayerNorm scale parameter γ alone, and that this direction lies exactly in the kernel of the post-final-norm centered activation covariance.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect but real. Persistent homology is used as a complementary lens for post-training dynamics, showing changes that may not be visible in task scores or aggregate behavior. The useful signal for pretraining researchers is that most topological reorganization appears to happen very early in fine-tuning, and different alignment objectives can induce separable representation trajectories even when their external behavior looks similar. This is informative for the pretrain–posttrain interface, but it is not yet a training recipe.","technical_approach":"The method treats hidden activations at each checkpoint as high-dimensional point clouds and computes persistent homology with a Vietoris–Rips filtration. The analysis focuses on H0 and H1, corresponding to connected components and loop-like structures. The pipeline fixes an evaluation set, extracts token-level latent activations, builds activation clouds, computes barcodes, summarizes them with topological descriptors, and compares trajectories across checkpoints, objectives, and model families. The models include TinyLlama-1.1B, Gemma-3-1B, Phi-3-mini-3.8B instruction-tuned, and Mistral-7B v0.1. Fine-tuning is done with LoRA on alignment objectives derived from Anthropic HH-RLHF. For the 1B models, checkpoints are saved every 50 optimization steps up to 300 steps, with an additional dense early-stage analysis every 5 steps; larger models use longer schedules. The central empirical finding is a transient peak of topological activity early in training, followed by rapid stabilization. Helpful, harmless, and mixed objectives produce distinguishable topological trajectories. The initial model state also matters: instruction-tuned and pretrained models show qualitatively different evolution patterns. The main caveats are that the experiments use LoRA rather than full fine-tuning, and the link between topological descriptors and downstream behavioral quality remains indirect.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. 的有效目标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why LLMs fail at RTL and Verilog code generation, and which failures can be reduced by SFT, RL, or test-time sampling. The focus is not only benchmark accuracy, but a decomposition of failures into syntax, static semantics, recoverable functional mistakes, and deeper model-specific knowledge gaps.","importance":"The useful lesson for pretraining and post-training research is that optimization can make a model produce code that compiles, while still failing to model hardware behavior. RTL requires parallel signal-flow reasoning and temporal circuit semantics, which are weakly represented in general programming data. The paper shows that SFT and RL can reduce surface errors but may expose or increase deeper functional failures. This has direct implications for code pretraining data coverage, domain-specific corpus construction, verifier design, and the limits of RL when the base model lacks the relevant domain knowledge.","technical_approach":"The authors propose a four-level fault taxonomy aligned with the HDL compilation and verification pipeline. L1 covers syntactic errors, where the generated code is outside the Verilog/SystemVerilog grammar. L2 covers static semantic errors, where the code parses but violates constraints enforced by elaboration, linting, or synthesis, such as invalid procedural assignments, net/register misuse, or port declaration issues. L3 covers code that passes compilation but fails functional testbenches. L3 is further divided into L3S and L3U. L3S errors are functionally wrong but recoverable through sampling or alternative outputs. L3U errors are unsolved by the model under the tested setup and are treated as evidence of a model-specific knowledge gap. The reported ceiling is 90.8% initial pass rate for the best frontier model, with a remaining 4.2% L3U floor. Across training paradigms, L3U errors remain in the 4–17% range, suggesting that these errors are not removed by simple sampling or surface-level optimization. SFT and RL reduce L1 and L2 errors but shift more failures into L3, meaning the models learn to generate compilable Verilog before they learn correct circuit behavior. RTL-specialized models show relatively high L3S rates, around 11–12%, which makes them more responsive to best-of-N sampling.
